The petitioner has been under detention pursuant to the order dated June 5, 1990 passed by the District Magistrate with a view to preventing him "from doing any such work which is prejudicial for the maintenance of public order". The grounds of detention were furnished to the detenu in time. Therein it was stated inter alia: "That you along with one companion on February 12, 1990 about 7.45 indiscriminately fired and murdered Dr D1 who was connected with a Nursing Home when she was returning from Dr D2's dental clinic situated at a busy and crowded road after the dental treatment along with her sister P1, with the result that the people started running here and there and were scared. In this connection a case was registered in Police Station and after its investigation charge-sheet has been submitted in the court against P2." It was stated" that the brutal murder has created a feeling of insecurity in public in general and among doctors in particular". The grounds of detention further said that the detenu was in District Jail in connection with a criminal case and he was on bail. But he got his bail cancelled on February 14, 1990 and was trying for his bail in another criminal case and his bail application was under consideration.
Podnosilac zahtjeva se nalazi u pritvoru na osnovu rješenja od 5. juna 1990. godine, koje je donio okružni magistrat u cilju da ga spriječi „da obavlja bilo kakav posao koji je štetan za održavanje javnog reda“. Razlozi za pritvor uručeni su pritvoreniku blagovremeno. U njima je, između ostalog, navedeno: „Da ste vi, zajedno sa jednim pratiocem, 12. februara 1990. godine oko 7.45 nasumično pucali i ubili dr D1, koja je bila povezana sa jednom klinikom, dok se, nakon stomatološkog liječenja, zajedno sa svojom sestrom P1 vraćala iz stomatološke ordinacije dr D2, koja se nalazi na prometnom i prepunom putu, usljed čega su ljudi počeli da bježe na sve strane i bili su uplašeni. S tim u vezi, u policijskoj stanici je registrovan predmet i nakon sprovedene istrage sudu je podnijet optužni akt protiv P2.“ Navedeno je „da je brutalno ubistvo stvorilo osjećaj nesigurnosti u javnosti uopšte, a posebno među ljekarima“. U razlozima za pritvor dalje je navedeno da se pritvorenik nalazio u okružnom zatvoru u vezi sa jednim krivičnim predmetom i da je bio na slobodi uz jemstvo. Međutim, on je 14. februara 1990. godine postigao da mu se jemstvo ukine i nastojao je da dobije jemstvo u drugom krivičnom predmetu, a njegov zahtjev za jemstvo bio je u postupku razmatranja.
queries · 00012
This is an appeal with a certificate granted by the judicial Commissioner. P1-hereinafter referred to as the respondent'-was appointed a constable in the Police Force of L1 by the Superintendent of Police, L2 by order dated April 18, 1954. The employment was temporary and was liable to be terminated with one month's notice. On December 6, 1957, the Superintendent of Police, informed the respondent that his services "'will be terminated with effect from 6-1-58 A. M." The respondent presented an appeal to the Chief Commissioner against the order of termination. By letter dated April 11, 1958 the respondent was informed that as he was "an Ex convict for theft, nothing can be done for him". In reply to another application addressed to the Chief Commissioner the respondent was informed by letter dated May 26, 1958, that he was already informed in connection with his previous appeal that as he was "'an Ex convict in a case of theft" he "cannot be reemployed by the Administration." The respondent then filed in the Court of the Judicial Commissioner, L1, a petition for a writ praying for a writ declaring that the order of the Superintendent of Police terminating his service was "illegal" and for a writ of mandamus or a writ of certiorari directing the Chief Commissioner not to enforce the said order and for an order reinstating him in the Police Force of the L1 Administration with retros pective effect. The L1 Administration submitted in rejoinder that the respondent being a temporary employee of the Police Force, his services were lawfully terminated. The Judicial Commissioner of L1 held that the respondent was a temporary employee, but the order terminating the respondent's employment was invalid for it infringed the constitutional guarantee of protection of public servants which applied to temporary as well as permanent public servants. In the view of the judicial Commissioner, termination of employment of a temporary servant  will not per se be treated as a punishment of dismissal or removal, but it is open to the Court even if an order merely of termination of employment of a temporary employee is passed to ascertain whether the order was intended to be of termination simpliciter or of dismissal entailing penal consequences, and that the order dated April 11, 1958, of the Chief Commissioner passed in appeal clearly indicated that the order of the Superintendent of Police was one imposing penalty. He observed: "This reply (dated April 11, 1958) will clearly indicate that though the Superintendent of Police purported to terminate his service, he meant to dismiss the petitioner from am service as a punishment on the ground that he was an ex-convict and that it was intended that he should not be reappointed in future in any department of the Government. Thus it cannot be gainsaid that the termination was in fact a punishment for previous misconduct debarring the petitioner from being, employed even in the future, and that in passing the innocuous order (dated December 6, 1957Annexure D), the Superintendent was really camouflaging his real intention. The real intention came to light, perhaps as the result of an oversight in communicating the orders in appeal to the petitioner".
Ovo je žalba izjavljena na osnovu potvrde koju je izdao sudski komesar. P1 – u daljem tekstu: protivna strana – postavljen je za policajca u policijskim snagama L1 rješenjem načelnika policije, L2, od 18. aprila 1954. godine. Zaposlenje je bilo privremeno i moglo je prestati uz otkazni rok od mjesec dana. Načelnik policije je 6. decembra 1957. godine obavijestio protivnu stranu da će njegova služba „prestati sa dejstvom od 6-1-58 prije podne“. Protivna strana je izjavila žalbu glavnom komesaru protiv rješenja o prestanku radnog odnosa. Dopisom od 11. aprila 1958. godine protivna strana je obaviještena da se, budući da je „ranije osuđivan za krađu, ništa ne može učiniti za njega“. Kao odgovor na drugi zahtjev upućen glavnom komesaru, protivna strana je dopisom od 26. maja 1958. godine obaviještena da je već obaviještena u vezi sa svojom prethodnom žalbom da, budući da je „ranije osuđivan u predmetu krađe“, on „ne može biti ponovo zaposlen u Administraciji“. Protivna strana je potom pred Sudom sudskog komesara, L1, podnijela ustavnu tužbu (writ petition) kojom je tražila izdavanje naloga (writ) kojim bi se utvrdilo da je rješenje načelnika policije o prestanku njegove službe „nezakonito“, kao i izdavanje naloga mandamus ili naloga certiorari kojim bi se glavnom komesaru naložilo da ne izvršava navedeno rješenje, te rješenja o njegovom vraćanju na rad u policijske snage Administracije L1 sa retroaktivnim dejstvom. Administracija L1 je u odgovoru navela da je, budući da je protivna strana bila privremeno zaposlena u policijskim snagama, njena služba zakonito prestala. Sudski komesar L1 je utvrdio da je protivna strana bila privremeno zaposlena, ali da je rješenje o prestanku radnog odnosa protivne strane nevažeće jer je povrijedilo ustavnu garanciju zaštite službenih lica, koja se primjenjuje kako na privremena, tako i na stalna službena lica. Po mišljenju sudskog komesara, prestanak radnog odnosa privremeno zaposlenog lica neće se per se smatrati kaznom otpuštanja ili razrješenja, ali je sud ovlašćen, čak i kada je doneseno rješenje kojim se samo utvrđuje prestanak radnog odnosa privremeno zaposlenog lica, da ispita da li je rješenje trebalo da predstavlja prestanak radnog odnosa simpliciter ili otpuštanje koje povlači kaznene posljedice, i da je rješenje glavnog komesara od 11. aprila 1958. godine, doneseno po žalbi, jasno ukazivalo da je rješenje načelnika policije bilo rješenje kojim se izriče kazna. On je konstatovao: „Ovaj odgovor (od 11. aprila 1958. godine) jasno ukazuje na to da je načelnik policije, iako je formalno prekinuo njegovu službu, namjeravao da podnosioca zahtjeva otpusti iz službe kao kaznu po osnovu toga što je ranije osuđivan i da je namjera bila da on u budućnosti ne bude ponovo postavljen ni u jednom odjeljenju Vlade. Prema tome, ne može se poreći da je prestanak radnog odnosa zapravo bio kazna za ranije nedolično ponašanje kojom se podnosiocu zahtjeva onemogućava da bude zaposlen čak i u budućnosti, i da je donošenjem bezazlenog rješenja (od 6. decembra 1957. godine, Prilog D) načelnik zapravo prikrivao svoju stvarnu namjeru. Stvarna namjera je izašla na vidjelo, možda kao posljedica previda prilikom dostavljanja rješenja donesenih po žalbi podnosiocu zahtjeva“.
queries · 00013
P1 is before us being aggrieved by and dissatisfied with the judgment and order dated 17.10.2005 passed by a Division Bench of the High Court. Appellant herein along with P2 (Accused No.1), P3 (Accused No. 3) and P4 (Accused No.4) were tried for committing the murder of one P5. P5 was an accused in a case of murder of the father of the appellant and accused No. 3. Allegedly, when cremation was taking place, the appellant took a vow to take revenge of murder of his father. P5 (deceased) on or about 13.02.1991 at about 11.00 a.m. was going to L1 on a motorcycle. He was accompanied by P6 (PW-4). When they were at distance of about 3 k.m. from P1, the accused persons who were in a Maruti van parked the vehicle by the side of road got down. The motorcycle was stopped by Accused Nos. 2, 3 and 4. P3 (Accused No. 3) is said to have caught hold P5 and P6 (Accused No.2) and P4 (Accused No. 4) inflicted stab injuries with knives. An attempt to rescue the deceased by PW-4 resulted in a threat to him, whereupon he started running towards Jalgaon. Bhaulal also tried to save himself by running away from the said place. He was chased by Accused Nos. 2 and 3 and was again assaulted with knives. PW-4 immediately went to the Taluka Police Station on a vehicle of a passer by. A First Information Report was lodged at about 11.45 a.m. P5 was taken to the hospital in a tractor. At about 12.45 p.m. he died. At the trial, the prosecution examined 17 witnesses. P6 (PW-4) and P7 (PW-5) were examined as eye- witnesses to the occurrence. We have noticed hereinbefore that PW-4 was the informant. PW-5 was the driver of the Maruti van, which was taken on hire by the accused persons. They had gone to L2 and L3. The learned Trial Judge upon considering the evidence brought on record convicted all the accused persons. The High Court by reason of the impugned judgment in the criminal appeal filed by the accused persons, however, set aside the conviction and sentence of Accused No.1. Accused Nos. 2, 3 and 4. Accused Nos. 2 and 3 were also convicted under Section 341 read with Section 34 IPC. Accused No. 2 was further convicted under Section 506 IPC. Indisputably, P1 preferred an appeal before this Court against the said judgment of conviction and sentence passed by the High Court.
P1 se obratio ovom sudu, pogođen i nezadovoljan presudom i rješenjem od 17. 10. 2005. godine, koje je donijelo vijeće (Division Bench) Visokog suda. Žalilac je, zajedno sa P2 (optuženi br. 1), P3 (optuženi br. 3) i P4 (optuženi br. 4), bio izveden pred sud zbog izvršenja ubistva izvjesnog P5. P5 je bio optuženi u predmetu ubistva oca žalioca i optuženog br. 3. Navodno je žalilac, dok je trajala kremacija, dao zavjet da će se osvetiti za ubistvo svog oca. P5 (pokojni) se 13. 02. 1991. godine ili približno tog dana, oko 11.00 časova prije podne, kretao motociklom prema L1. Pratio ga je P6 (PW-4). Kada su se nalazili na udaljenosti od oko 3 km od P1, optužena lica, koja su se nalazila u kombiju marke Maruti, parkirala su vozilo pored puta i izašla. Optuženi br. 2, 3 i 4 zaustavili su motocikl. Navodi se da je P3 (optuženi br. 3) uhvatio P5, a da su P6 (optuženi br. 2) i P4 (optuženi br. 4) nanijeli ubodne povrede noževima. Pokušaj PW-4 da spase pokojnog doveo je do prijetnje upućene njemu, nakon čega je on počeo da bježi prema Jalgaonu. Bhaulal je takođe pokušao da se spase bježeći sa navedenog mjesta. Optuženi br. 2 i 3 su ga progonili i ponovo ga napali noževima. PW-4 je odmah otišao do policijske stanice Taluka vozilom jednog prolaznika. Krivična prijava (FIR) podnesena je oko 11.45 časova prije podne. P5 je prevezen u bolnicu traktorom. Oko 12.45 časova poslije podne je preminuo. Na suđenju je tužilaštvo saslušalo 17 svjedoka. P6 (PW-4) i P7 (PW-5) saslušani su kao očevici događaja. Već smo naprijed konstatovali da je PW-4 bio podnosilac prijave. PW-5 je bio vozač kombija marke Maruti, koji su optužena lica unajmila. Oni su bili otišli u L2 i L3. Uvaženi sudija prvostepenog suda je, nakon razmatranja dokaza izvedenih u postupku, oglasio krivim sva optužena lica. Visoki sud je, međutim, pobijanom presudom donesenom po krivičnoj žalbi koju su izjavila optužena lica, stavio van snage osuđujuću presudu i kaznu optuženom br. 1. Optuženi br. 2, 3 i 4. Optuženi br. 2 i 3 oglašeni su krivim i za krivično djelo iz člana 341 u vezi sa članom 34 Krivičnog zakonika Indije. Optuženi br. 2 je dodatno oglašen krivim za krivično djelo iz člana 506 Krivičnog zakonika. Nesporno je da je P1 izjavio žalbu ovom sudu protiv navedene osuđujuće presude i kazne koju je izrekao Visoki sud.
queries · 00014
The appellants are five in number and they have filed this appeal as of right. They were acquitted by the trial court but convicted by the High Court on appeal filed by the State, besides the offence of rioting. On the first count they were each sentenced to imprisonment for life besides fine and to lesser sentences for the lesser offence. The gist of the case against them is that all the five accused formed themselves into an unlawful assembly on the evening of 24-10-1985 and lay in ambush waiting for P1 (deceased) to come through the road and on seeing P1 they all launched an attack with weapons like gandasa, takua and ghope. The wife of P1 (P2, PW 10) who accompanied her husband made a bid to rescue her husband from the onslaughts of the assailants, but she too was attacked by them. P1 died at the spot. PW 10 (P2) furnished first information to the police on the same evening on the basis of which FIR was registered by the police. Subsequently all the five accused were arrested and on the strength of the information elicited from Ist accused P3 and third accused P4 weapons of offences were recovered from concealed places. The most important witness in this case is PW 10 (P2). The importance of her evidence is that no court can possibly hold that she would not have witnessed the occurrence, for she herself sustained as many as eight injuries, some of them serious, during the occurrence. The fact that she gave the police prompt information which has reached the Magistrate on the very same night adds further credence to her version. Added to that, she narrated the incident to PW 12 and PW 13 (a chowkidar) who reached the spot soon after the occurrence. The trial court did not place reliance on the evidence of PW 10 (P2) principally on the ground that her evidence did not agree with the testimony of PW 11 (JoP4). It must be pointed out that JoP4 was cited as an eyewitness and he turned hostile and the Public Prosecutor confronted him with his own previous statements and contradicted him. It is not safe to jettison the evidence of a witness like PW 10 (P2) as important as she is in the syndrome of the facts of this case, with the aid of a hostile witness's stance in the Court. Such a course adopted by the trial court had been rightly deprecated by the High Court. The defence version was that PW 10 would have possibly sustained those injuries at some other place and at some other time and not when she was accompanying her husband at the place of occurrence. Such a suggestion is too unconvincing and very far-fetched. No court can give any weight to such a theory. Even the defence admitted that PW 10 sustained so many injuries as described by PW 1 Dr D1 in his evidence. We are totally disinclined to countenance the contention that PW 10 would have absolved those who have attacked her and turned against the five accused falsely. Such a theory will not fit in with logic and common sense. But unfortunately that version was found acceptable to the trial court and therefore the High Court has rightly repelled it.
Žalilaca je pet i oni su ovu žalbu izjavili po osnovu zakonskog prava na žalbu. Prvostepeni sud ih je oslobodio, ali ih je Visoki sud, po žalbi koju je izjavila Država, oglasio krivim, pored krivičnog djela nereda. Po prvoj tački optužbe svakom od njih izrečena je kazna doživotnog zatvora, pored novčane kazne, kao i blaže kazne za lakše krivično djelo. Suština optužbe protiv njih je da je svih pet optuženih uveče 24. 10. 1985. godine obrazovalo nedozvoljeno okupljanje i postavilo zasjedu čekajući da P1 (pokojni) naiđe putem, te da su, kada su ugledali P1, svi izvršili napad oružjem kao što su gandasa, takua i ghope. Supruga P1 (P2, PW 10), koja je pratila svog muža, pokušala je da spase muža od napada napadača, ali su i nju napali. P1 je preminuo na licu mjesta. PW 10 (P2) je iste večeri dala policiji prvo obavještenje, na osnovu kojeg je policija registrovala krivičnu prijavu (FIR). Nakon toga je svih pet optuženih uhapšeno i na osnovu informacija dobijenih od prvooptuženog P3 i trećeoptuženog P4 sredstva izvršenja krivičnog djela pronađena su na skrivenim mjestima. Najvažniji svjedok u ovom predmetu je PW 10 (P2). Značaj njenog iskaza je u tome što nijedan sud ne bi mogao da zaključi da ona nije bila očevidac događaja, jer je i sama tokom događaja zadobila čak osam povreda, od kojih su neke bile teške. Činjenica da je odmah dala obavještenje policiji, koje je stiglo do magistrata iste noći, dodatno povećava vjerodostojnost njene verzije. Pored toga, ona je opisala događaj svjedocima PW 12 i PW 13 (čuvar), koji su stigli na lice mjesta ubrzo nakon događaja. Prvostepeni sud nije poklonio vjeru iskazu PW 10 (P2) prvenstveno po osnovu toga što njen iskaz nije bio u skladu sa iskazom PW 11 (JoP4). Treba istaći da je JoP4 bio predložen kao očevidac i da je postao svjedok koji je promijenio iskaz, te da mu je javni tužilac predočio njegove ranije izjave i ukazao na protivrječnosti. Nije opravdano odbaciti iskaz svjedoka kao što je PW 10 (P2), koja je toliko važna u sklopu činjenica ovog predmeta, oslanjanjem na stav koji je pred sudom zauzeo svjedok koji je promijenio iskaz. Takav pristup prvostepenog suda Visoki sud je s pravom osudio. Verzija odbrane je bila da je PW 10 možda zadobila te povrede na nekom drugom mjestu i u neko drugo vrijeme, a ne dok je pratila svog muža na mjestu događaja. Takva sugestija je krajnje neuvjerljiva i veoma natjerana. Nijedan sud ne može pridati nikakav značaj takvoj teoriji. Čak je i odbrana priznala da je PW 10 zadobila onoliko povreda koliko je opisao PW 1 dr D1 u svom iskazu. Potpuno smo neskloni da prihvatimo tvrdnju da bi PW 10 oslobodila odgovornosti one koji su je napali i lažno se okrenula protiv petorice optuženih. Takva teorija se ne uklapa u logiku i zdrav razum. Međutim, nažalost, prvostepeni sud je tu verziju prihvatio, pa ju je Visoki sud s pravom odbacio.

facts of the matter, as is evident from the present Writ Petition challenging an order of detention dated 1st March, 2001 it appears that the petitioner is presently confined in Central Prison and it is this detention which the petitioner contended is without the authority of law and constitute an infringement of his guaranteed fundamental rights. The reason for detention has been and as recorded by the Department is that the Bill of Entry No.235337 dated 19.7.2000 was filed in the name of E Enterprises for clearance of 300 numbers of ACER CD ROM drive 50X by Customs House Agents, C1. According to the Department this Bill of Entry was filed in the name of E Enterprises but the latter expressly intimated the department stating that they did not place any order for import purposes. The department made an investigation and the goods were seized. The total CIF value according to the department was Rs.43,53,189/- and Rs.57,87,200/- was the market value. The petitioner appeared before the Customs Department on 24.7.2000 and the officers detained him and obtained the statements and was subsequently arrested on 25.7.2000 for an offence. The principal allegation against the petitioner/detenu being misdeclaration in the Bill of Entry. The petitioner/detenu however was remanded to judicial custody on 26.7.2000. Subsequently, the detenu was enlarged on bail by the learned Additional Chief Metropolitan Magistrate on 11.8.2000. The Department after the completion of investigation issued a show-cause notice.  Significantly, though the incident noticed above took place on 24.7.2000 and other important documents have come into existence immediately thereafter, the detaining authority did not pass the detention order immediately but only after a lapse of about seven months, i.e. on 1.3.2000. During this interregnum, however, the detenu admittedly did not indulge in any illegal activities and it is on this context P1, learned advocate appearing in support of the petition with his usual eloquence contended that the incident of 24th July, 2000 had become stale and irrelevant and it is too remote in point of time and as such question of there being any detention order on the basis thereof would not arise. P1 further contended upon reference to the fact situation as adverted herein before in this judgment that the detenu was arrested on 25.7.2000 for offences and was remanded to judicial custody on 26.7.2000. The detenu was however enlarged on bail by the learned Additional Chief Metropolitan Magistrate (EO.III) on 11.8.2000 and the Department after completing the investigation issued the required show-cause notice on 19.9.2000. The factual score thus lends a substantial credence to the submissions of P1 as regards the charges being too stale to be taken recourse to in the matter of issuance of the order of detention on 1st March, 2001 more so, having regard to the admitted factum of non-involvement of the detenu in any illegal activity and thus consequently too remote as well in point of time to be the basis of an order of detention. It is in elaboration of his submissions P1 contended that once the show-cause notice has been issued, there cannot be any manner of doubt that the investigation is complete, but in the contextual facts the detaining authority has failed to apply its mind as regards the issue of unreasonable delay in passing the order of detention.
Činjenično stanje predmeta, kako proizlazi iz ove ustavne tužbe (writ petition) kojom se osporava rješenje o pritvoru od 1. marta 2001. godine, ukazuje na to da se podnosilac zahtjeva trenutno nalazi u Centralnom zatvoru i da je upravo taj pritvor onaj za koji je podnosilac zahtjeva tvrdio da je bez zakonskog ovlašćenja i da predstavlja povredu njegovih zajemčenih osnovnih prava. Razlog za pritvor, kako ga je evidentirala Uprava, jeste to što su carinski agenti, C1, podnijeli carinsku deklaraciju (Bill of Entry) br. 235337 od 19. jula 2000. godine na ime E Enterprises radi carinjenja 300 komada ACER CD ROM uređaja 50X. Prema navodima Uprave, ova carinska deklaracija podnesena je na ime E Enterprises, ali je potonji izričito obavijestio Upravu da nije izdao nikakvu narudžbu u svrhu uvoza. Uprava je sprovela istragu i roba je zaplijenjena. Ukupna CIF vrijednost, prema navodima Uprave, iznosila je Rs.43,53,189/-, a tržišna vrijednost Rs.57,87,200/-. Podnosilac zahtjeva pojavio se pred Carinskom upravom 24. jula 2000. godine, a službenici su ga zadržali i uzeli izjave, nakon čega je 25. jula 2000. godine uhapšen zbog krivičnog djela. Glavni navod protiv podnosioca zahtjeva/pritvorenog lica bio je netačno prijavljivanje u carinskoj deklaraciji. Podnosilac zahtjeva/pritvoreno lice je, međutim, 26. jula 2000. godine upućen u sudski pritvor. Nakon toga, uvaženi dodatni glavni gradski magistrat je 11. avgusta 2000. godine pustio pritvoreno lice na slobodu uz jemstvo. Uprava je po okončanju istrage izdala poziv za izjašnjenje (show-cause notice). Značajno je da, iako se gore navedeni događaj odigrao 24. jula 2000. godine, a drugi važni dokumenti nastali neposredno nakon toga, organ nadležan za određivanje pritvora nije odmah donio rješenje o pritvoru, već tek nakon proteka od oko sedam mjeseci, tj. 1. marta 2000. godine. Tokom tog perioda, međutim, pritvoreno lice nesporno nije učestvovalo ni u kakvim nezakonitim aktivnostima i u tom kontekstu je P1, uvaženi advokat koji je istupao u prilog zahtjevu, sa svojom uobičajenom rječitošću tvrdio da je događaj od 24. jula 2000. godine protekom vremena postao prevaziđen i irelevantan i da je vremenski suviše udaljen, te da stoga ne može biti riječi o bilo kakvom rješenju o pritvoru na osnovu njega. P1 je dalje, pozivajući se na činjenično stanje opisano ranije u ovoj presudi, tvrdio da je pritvoreno lice uhapšeno 25. jula 2000. godine zbog krivičnih djela i upućeno u sudski pritvor 26. jula 2000. godine. Uvaženi dodatni glavni gradski magistrat (EO.III) je, međutim, 11. avgusta 2000. godine pustio pritvoreno lice na slobodu uz jemstvo, a Uprava je po okončanju istrage izdala potrebni poziv za izjašnjenje 19. septembra 2000. godine. Činjenično stanje stoga daje značajnu vjerodostojnost navodima P1 u pogledu toga da su optužbe suviše davne da bi se na njih moglo pozvati prilikom donošenja rješenja o pritvoru 1. marta 2001. godine, tim prije imajući u vidu nespornu činjenicu da pritvoreno lice nije bilo umiješano ni u kakvu nezakonitu aktivnost, te da su posljedično i vremenski suviše udaljene da bi bile osnov za rješenje o pritvoru. Razrađujući svoje navode, P1 je tvrdio da, kada je jednom izdat poziv za izjašnjenje, ne može biti nikakve sumnje da je istraga okončana, ali da u konkretnim okolnostima organ nadležan za određivanje pritvora nije razmotrio pitanje nerazumnog kašnjenja u donošenju rješenja o pritvoru.
queries · 00017
These appeals involve a pure question of law as to whether an award by which residue assets of a partnership firm are distributed amongst the partners on dissolution of the partnership firm requires registration. Briefly the facts are that a partnership firm was constituted comprising of four persons belonging to the same family. Disputes and differences arose between the partners which were ultimately referred to arbitration. The arbitrators made an award on 2nd October, 1972. The award was challenged by way of objections filed under by some of the partners. The objection petition was contested by the other partners who prayed that the award be made a rule of the Court. The grounds of challenge to the award included misconduct on the part of the arbitrators as well as another ground that the award required registration. The trial Court accepted both the objections holding that there was misconduct on the part of the arbitrators as also that the award was required to be compulsorily registered and since it was not registered it was inadmissible in evidence. This decision of the trial court was challenged before the High Court by way of a Civil Revision filed. The High Court found that in the facts and circumstances of the case it could not be said that there was any legal misconduct on the part of the arbitrators. Thus the first ground of attack against the award was found to be unsustainable. However, the High Court accepted the finding of the trial Court on the second ground, that is, the award was required to be compulsorily registered. Since the award was unregistered, it could not be made a rule of the Court. Hence the present appeals.
Ove žalbe otvaraju čisto pravno pitanje da li arbitražna odluka kojom se preostala imovina ortačkog društva raspodjeljuje između ortaka po prestanku ortačkog društva zahtijeva registraciju. Ukratko, činjenice su sljedeće: osnovano je ortačko društvo koje su činila četiri lica iz iste porodice. Između ortaka su nastali sporovi i nesuglasice, koji su konačno upućeni na arbitražu. Arbitri su donijeli odluku 2. oktobra 1972. godine. Odluka je osporena prigovorima koje su podnijeli neki od ortaka. Prigovor su osporili ostali ortaci, koji su predložili da se odluka potvrdi kao odluka Suda (rule of the Court). Razlozi osporavanja odluke uključivali su nepropisno postupanje arbitara, kao i drugi razlog, da odluka zahtijeva registraciju. Prvostepeni sud je prihvatio oba prigovora, zaključivši da je bilo nepropisnog postupanja arbitara, kao i da je odluka morala biti obavezno registrovana, a budući da nije bila registrovana, bila je nedozvoljena kao dokaz. Ova odluka prvostepenog suda osporena je pred Visokim sudom putem zahtjeva za preispitivanje u parničnom postupku (revizija). Visoki sud je utvrdio da se, u činjeničnim okolnostima ovog predmeta, ne može reći da je bilo ikakvog pravno nepropisnog postupanja arbitara. Prema tome, utvrđeno je da je prvi razlog osporavanja odluke neodrživ. Međutim, Visoki sud je prihvatio utvrđenje prvostepenog suda u pogledu drugog razloga, odnosno da je odluka morala biti obavezno registrovana. Budući da odluka nije bila registrovana, nije se mogla potvrditi kao odluka Suda. Otuda ove žalbe.
queries · 00018
This appeal is preferred by the appellants against the judgment and order dated 31.08.2012 passed by the High Court whereby the High Court has allowed the appeal filed by the State and convicted all the appellants and sentenced them to undergo rigorous imprisonment for life and to pay a fine of Rs.10,000/- each. The brief facts of the case, as per the prosecution story, are that on 5.1.2001 at about 6:10 p.m. at  L1, the accused persons formed an unlawful assembly and in prosecution of the common object of such assembly, committed the murder of  P2.  P3, son of the deceased (PW-3) lodged an Ejahar about the incident at Police Station on 5.1.2001 at about 10:00 p.m. On receipt of the Ejahar, F.I.R. No.3 was registered by Police Station and started investigation. The police arrived at the place of occurrence and called the Executive Magistrate who prepared the inquest on the dead body and the inquest was sent for post-mortem examination to Civil Hospital. The police found one bag containing one dagger and two hand-made bombs lying near the dead body. After investigation, charge-sheet was submitted against the accused, the said charge-sheet was received by the Chief Judicial Magistrate by the Since Court the offence was of Sessions, the triable Chief Judicial Magistrate by his order dated 15.3.2002 committed the case to the Court of Sessions for trial. During the course of trial the prosecution examined 10 witnesses to bring home the charges levelled against the appellants. The defense adduced no evidence and took a plea of total denial. The Trial Court on a careful scrutiny of the evidence found that the statements of PW-4 & PW-5 were contradictory which created doubt as to the presence of these two witnesses at the place of occurrence. PW-1 deposed that about 6 months ago, when he was returning from the Pharmacy, he met P5 who said that his brother had been killed in the market, but he did not mention the name of any person. The incident took place in the market place where there were about 50 shops on both sides of the road. The Trial Court observed that if accused  P5 and  P6 appeared from the left and right, they must have come out of one of the shops on both sides of the road since PW-4 categorically stated that he had not seen the accused persons on the road while they were going towards the house of the deceased. But none of the shopkeepers, adjacent to the place of occurrence, came forward to depose that any occurrence as stated by PW-4 & PW-5 had taken place in front of their shops. PW-5 during cross-examination stated that he knew the names of two shopkeepers and they are P7 and P8. P8 (PW-2) did not state that the occurrence took place in front of his shop. PW-5 further stated during cross examination that the deceased was an accused in a murder case and had no explanation as to whether the deceased would move around having bombs and other weapons with him. The Trial Court drew the conclusion that the seized articles were belonging to the deceased persons. On analysis of the evidence the Trial Court decided that the evidence of PW-4 and PW-5 was full of contradictions on material particulars and as such the testimony of these witnesses did not inspire any confidence. Under the circumstances, the uncorroborated testimony of PW-4 and PW-5 by some independent eye witness could not be accepted to warrant the conviction of the accused persons. The High Court on the other hand overruled the decision of the Trial Court and convicted all the five accused and sentenced them to undergo rigorous imprisonment for life and to pay a fine of Rs.10,000/- each.
Ovu žalbu izjavili su žalioci protiv presude i rješenja od 31. avgusta 2012. godine koje je donio Visoki sud, kojima je Visoki sud usvojio žalbu koju je izjavila država i oglasio krivim sve žalioce i osudio ih na kaznu strogog doživotnog zatvora i novčanu kaznu od Rs.10,000/- svakome. Ukratko, činjenice predmeta, prema verziji optužbe, jesu sljedeće: 5. januara 2001. godine, oko 6:10 časova poslije podne, u L1, optuženi su obrazovali nedozvoljeno okupljanje i, u ostvarivanju zajedničkog cilja tog okupljanja, izvršili ubistvo P2. P3, sin pokojnog (PW-3), podnio je prijavu (Ejahar) o događaju u policijskoj stanici 5. januara 2001. godine oko 10:00 časova uveče. Po prijemu prijave (Ejahar), policijska stanica je registrovala krivičnu prijavu (F.I.R.) br. 3 i započela istragu. Policija je stigla na mjesto događaja i pozvala izvršnog magistrata, koji je sačinio zapisnik o uviđaju na lešu, a zapisnik o uviđaju je upućen u Civilnu bolnicu radi obdukcije. Policija je pored leša pronašla jednu torbu u kojoj su se nalazili jedan bodež i dvije ručno izrađene bombe. Nakon sprovedene istrage, protiv optuženih je podnesen optužni akt, koji je primio glavni sudski magistrat, a budući da je za suđenje za to krivično djelo bio nadležan Sesijski sud, glavni sudski magistrat je rješenjem od 15. marta 2002. godine uputio predmet Sesijskom sudu na suđenje. Tokom suđenja optužba je saslušala 10 svjedoka kako bi dokazala optužbe podignute protiv žalilaca. Odbrana nije izvela nikakve dokaze i u potpunosti je negirala optužbe. Prvostepeni sud je, nakon pažljive ocjene dokaza, utvrdio da su iskazi PW-4 i PW-5 bili protivrječni, što je izazvalo sumnju u prisustvo ova dva svjedoka na mjestu događaja. PW-1 je izjavio da je prije oko 6 mjeseci, kada se vraćao iz apoteke, sreo P5, koji mu je rekao da mu je brat ubijen na pijaci, ali nije pomenuo ime nijednog lica. Događaj se odigrao na pijaci, gdje je sa obje strane puta bilo oko 50 radnji. Prvostepeni sud je konstatovao da su, ako su se optuženi P5 i P6 pojavili s lijeve i desne strane, morali izaći iz neke od radnji sa obje strane puta, budući da je PW-4 kategorički izjavio da nije vidio optužene na putu dok su išli prema kući pokojnog. Međutim, nijedan od vlasnika radnji u blizini mjesta događaja nije se javio da svjedoči da se bilo kakav događaj, kako su ga opisali PW-4 i PW-5, odigrao ispred njihovih radnji. PW-5 je tokom unakrsnog ispitivanja izjavio da zna imena dva vlasnika radnji i da su to P7 i P8. P8 (PW-2) nije izjavio da se događaj odigrao ispred njegove radnje. PW-5 je dalje tokom unakrsnog ispitivanja izjavio da je pokojni bio okrivljeni u predmetu ubistva i nije imao objašnjenje da li bi se pokojni kretao okolo noseći sa sobom bombe i drugo oružje. Prvostepeni sud je izveo zaključak da su oduzeti predmeti pripadali pokojnom. Analizom dokaza prvostepeni sud je odlučio da je iskaz PW-4 i PW-5 pun protivrječnosti u pogledu bitnih pojedinosti i da stoga iskazi ovih svjedoka ne ulivaju nikakvo povjerenje. U tim okolnostima, iskaz PW-4 i PW-5 koji nije potkrijepljen nekim nezavisnim očevicem nije se mogao prihvatiti kao osnov za osuđujuću presudu protiv optuženih. Visoki sud je, s druge strane, preinačio odluku prvostepenog suda i oglasio krivim svih pet optuženih i osudio ih na kaznu strogog doživotnog zatvora i novčanu kaznu od Rs.10,000/- svakome.
queries · 00019
This appeal by special leave is directed against the Judgment rendered by a High Court confirming the conviction of the appellant for the offences punishable. The High Court by the impugned judgment confirmed the judgment passed by the Principal Sessions Judge convicting the appellant and sentencing him to suffer imprisonment for life and rigorous imprisonment for seven years, respectively, and further to pay a fine of Rs.1,000/- each and in default, to suffer simple imprisonment for a period of six months each. In the nutshell, the prosecution version which led to the trial of the appellant is as under: On the intervening night of 7th/8th April, 2002 appellant went to the house of the deceased in a village with an intention to end the life of the deceased and knocked at the door of the deceased which was opened by the wife of the deceased,  P1 (PW-3) and the appellant-accused all of a sudden hacked and attempted to kill her by inflicting severe injuries on her body, as a result of which she fell down and then he rushed towards the deceased who was sleeping and hacked him by inflicting severe injuries. The appellant after committing the offence escaped from the place of occurrence by bolting the door from outside. The daughter of the deceased  P2 (PW-4) aged about 9 years raised hue and cry upon which the neighbours opened the door from outside and informed  P1 Gangaraju (PW1) who is a close relation of the deceased who thereafter informed Police Station and lodged first information report at about 4.00 a.m. on 8th April, 2002. A case was registered against unknown persons. The prosecution, in order to establish its case, examined altogether 14 witnesses. The trial court upon appreciation of the evidence found the appellant guilty and sentenced him to suffer imprisonment for life and rigorous imprisonment for a period of seven years, respectively. The trial court mainly relied upon the evidence of PW-3 and PW-4. The High Court vide its judgment dated 20th August, 2007 dismissed the criminal appeal filed by the appellant and accordingly confirmed the judgment of the trial court. The High Court too relied upon the evidence of PW-3 and PW-4. Both courts below found that the appellant attacked the deceased with sharp edged weapon resulting in his death. The courts below also found the appellant- accused attempted to kill PW-3 by inflicting severe injuries on her body.
Ova žalba po posebnoj dozvoli usmjerena je protiv presude koju je donio Visoki sud, kojom je potvrđena osuđujuća presuda protiv žalioca za kažnjiva krivična djela. Visoki sud je pobijanom presudom potvrdio presudu koju je donio glavni sudija Sesijskog suda, kojom je žalilac oglašen krivim i osuđen na kaznu doživotnog zatvora, odnosno strogi zatvor u trajanju od sedam godina, kao i na novčanu kaznu od Rs.1,000/- za svako djelo, a u slučaju neplaćanja, na obični zatvor u trajanju od šest mjeseci za svako djelo. Ukratko, verzija optužbe koja je dovela do suđenja žaliocu je sljedeća: U noći između 7. i 8. aprila 2002. godine žalilac je otišao do kuće pokojnog u jednom selu u namjeri da oduzme život pokojnom i pokucao je na vrata pokojnog, koja je otvorila supruga pokojnog, P1 (PW-3), a žalilac-optuženi ju je iznenada isjekao i pokušao da je ubije nanoseći joj teške povrede po tijelu, usljed čega je pala, a zatim je pojurio prema pokojnom, koji je spavao, i isjekao ga nanoseći mu teške povrede. Žalilac je nakon izvršenja krivičnog djela pobjegao s mjesta događaja, zatvorivši vrata spolja zasunom. Kćerka pokojnog, P2 (PW-4), stara oko 9 godina, podigla je galamu, nakon čega su susjedi otvorili vrata spolja i obavijestili P1 Gangaraju (PW1), bliskog srodnika pokojnog, koji je potom obavijestio policijsku stanicu i podnio krivičnu prijavu oko 4.00 časova ujutro 8. aprila 2002. godine. Predmet je registrovan protiv nepoznatih lica. Optužba je, radi dokazivanja svojih navoda, saslušala ukupno 14 svjedoka. Prvostepeni sud je, nakon ocjene dokaza, oglasio žalioca krivim i osudio ga na kaznu doživotnog zatvora, odnosno strogi zatvor u trajanju od sedam godina. Prvostepeni sud se uglavnom oslonio na iskaze PW-3 i PW-4. Visoki sud je presudom od 20. avgusta 2007. godine odbio krivičnu žalbu koju je izjavio žalilac i shodno tome potvrdio presudu prvostepenog suda. I Visoki sud se oslonio na iskaze PW-3 i PW-4. Oba niža suda su utvrdila da je žalilac napao pokojnog oštrim oružjem, što je za posljedicu imalo njegovu smrt. Niži sudovi su takođe utvrdili da je žalilac-optuženi pokušao da ubije PW-3 nanoseći joj teške povrede po tijelu.
queries · 00020
Challenge in this appeal is to the judgment of a learned Single Judge of the High Court by which two Writ Petitions filed by the respondent were disposed of. The controversy lies within a very narrow compass. Before dealing with the rival contentions the factual background needs to be noted. Respondent was appointed on 3.9.1980 as a daily-rated worker in the Horticulture Department of the State. In the Writ Petition the prayer was for regularization as a clerk on completion of ten years of service on daily wages basis. It is to be noted that the union of the employees had moved the Labour Court for regularization of all daily wagers. The same was adjudicated by the Industrial Disputes Tribunal. A reference was made to the Labour Court and the State filed its response questioning maintainability of the reference. Initially the Labour Court had decided in favour of the workers but on a Writ Petition being filed, the High Court held in favour of the State holding that the claim for regularization was not maintainable. It was noted that no appointment order was issued and the case of the respondent was not sponsored by the employment exchange. It was also noted that the claim for equal work for equal pay was not maintainable as daily-rated persons were not required to perform duties at par with those in regular service and they did not also fulfil the procedure at the time of recruitment. Two Writ Petitions were filed; in one the challenge was to the order of the Industrial Disputes Tribunal while the Writ Petition to which this Appeal relates to the Award by the Labour Court. It is to be noted that the Labour Court had observed that the employer had regularized the respondent as a Chowkidar with effect from 5.7.1997 which was refused by him. Thereafter the engagement as daily wager was terminated. This order was challenged before the Industrial Disputes Tribunal which was dismissed. However, as noted above the High Court has remanded the matter to the Tribunal. The High Court in the impugned order held that the approach of the Labour Court was wrong as it has introduced concepts which are unnecessary. It was noted by the High Court that there was no dispute that the respondent was employed as a clerk. Learned counsel for the respondents submitted that the question whether the appointment was as a clerk has been concluded by an earlier order of the High Court which has become final and, therefore, the present appeal is misconceived.
Predmet ove žalbe je presuda uvaženog sudije pojedinca Visokog suda kojom je odlučeno o dvije ustavne tužbe (writ petition) koje je podnijela protivna strana. Sporno pitanje je veoma uskog obima. Prije razmatranja suprotstavljenih navoda strana potrebno je izložiti činjenično stanje. Protivna strana je 3. septembra 1980. godine postavljena kao radnik sa dnevnom zaradom u Odjeljenju za hortikulturu države. U ustavnoj tužbi zahtjev se odnosio na prevođenje u stalni radni odnos na radnom mjestu službenika (clerk) po navršetku deset godina rada po osnovu dnevne zarade. Treba napomenuti da se sindikat zaposlenih obratio Sudu za radne sporove radi prevođenja svih radnika sa dnevnom zaradom u stalni radni odnos. O tome je odlučio Tribunal za industrijske sporove. Predmet je upućen Sudu za radne sporove, a Država je podnijela odgovor u kojem je osporila dozvoljenost upućivanja. Sud za radne sporove je prvobitno odlučio u korist radnika, ali je, po podnijetoj ustavnoj tužbi, Visoki sud odlučio u korist države, utvrdivši da zahtjev za prevođenje u stalni radni odnos nije dozvoljen. Konstatovano je da nije izdato rješenje o postavljenju i da kandidaturu protivne strane nije uputio zavod za zapošljavanje. Konstatovano je i da zahtjev za jednaku zaradu za jednak rad nije dozvoljen, jer lica sa dnevnom zaradom nijesu bila dužna da obavljaju poslove u istoj mjeri kao lica u stalnom radnom odnosu, a nijesu ni ispunila propisani postupak u vrijeme zapošljavanja. Podnijete su dvije ustavne tužbe; u jednoj je osporeno rješenje Tribunala za industrijske sporove, dok se ustavna tužba na koju se odnosi ova žalba odnosi na odluku (Award) Suda za radne sporove. Treba napomenuti da je Sud za radne sporove konstatovao da je poslodavac preveo protivnu stranu u stalni radni odnos na radnom mjestu čuvara (Chowkidar) sa dejstvom od 5. jula 1997. godine, što je on odbio. Nakon toga je prestalo njegovo angažovanje u svojstvu radnika sa dnevnom zaradom. To rješenje je osporeno pred Tribunalom za industrijske sporove, koji je zahtjev odbio. Međutim, kako je prethodno navedeno, Visoki sud je vratio predmet tribunalu na ponovni postupak. Visoki sud je u osporenom rješenju utvrdio da je pristup Suda za radne sporove bio pogrešan, jer je uveo pojmove koji nijesu neophodni. Visoki sud je konstatovao da nije bilo spora da je protivna strana bila zaposlena kao službenik. Uvaženi zastupnik protivne strane je istakao da je pitanje da li je postavljenje izvršeno na radno mjesto službenika riješeno ranijim rješenjem Visokog suda koje je postalo pravosnažno i da je, stoga, ova žalba neosnovana.
queries · 00021
Assailing the legal acceptability of the judgment and order passed by the High Court where it has given endorsement to the judgment passed by the learned Additional Sessions Judge wherein the learned trial Judge had found the appellants guilty of the offences and imposed the sentence of rigorous imprisonment of seven years and a fine of Rs.1,000/- on the first score, five years rigorous imprisonment and a fine of Rs.1,000/- on the second score, eighteen months rigorous imprisonment and a fine of Rs.500/- on the third count and six months rigorous imprisonment and a fine of Rs.250/- on the fourth count with the default clause for the fine amount in respect of each of the offences. The learned trial Judge stipulated that all the sentences shall be concurrent. Filtering the unnecessary details, the prosecution case, in brief, is that the marriage between the appellant No. 1 and deceased  P1, sister of the informant, PW-2, was solemnized on 24.9.1997. After the marriage the deceased stayed with her husband and the mother-in-law, the appellant No.2 herein, at the matrimonial home. In the wedlock, two children, one son and a daughter were born. On 11.9.2001, the informant, brother of the deceased, got a telephonic call from the accused No. 1 that his sister  P1 had committed suicide. On receipt of the telephone call came along with his friend, P2, PW-20, and at that juncture, the husband of  P1,  P3, informed that the deceased was fed up with the constant ill-health of her children and the said frustration had led her to commit suicide by tying a 'dupatta' around her neck. The brother of the deceased did not believe the version of  P3, and lodged an FIR alleging that the husband and the mother-in-law of the deceased, after the marriage, had been constantly asking for dowry of Rs.2 lacs from the father of the deceased, but as the said demand could not be satisfied due to the financial condition of the father, the husband and his mother started ill-treating her in the matrimonial home and being unable to tolerate the physical and mental torture she was compelled to commit suicide. Be it noted, as the death was unnatural, the police had sent the dead body for post mortem and the doctor conducting the autopsy opined that the death was due to suicide. After the criminal law was set in motion on the base of the FIR lodged by the brother, the investigating officer examined number of witnesses and after completing all the formalities laid the charge sheet before the competent Court, who, in turn, committed the matter to the Court of Session. The accused persons denied the allegations and claimed to be tried. The prosecution, in order to establish the charges levelled against the accused persons, examined 22 witnesses and got marked number of documents. The defence chose not to adduce any evidence. 4. The learned trial Judge principally posed four questions, namely, whether the accused persons had inflicted unbearable torture on the deceased as well as caused mental harassment to make themselves liable for punishment; whether the material brought on record established the offence; whether the physical and mental torture on the deceased compelled her to commit suicide on 11.9.2001 as a consequence of which the accused persons had become liable to be convicted; and whether the accused persons had demanded a sum of Rs.2 lacs towards dowry from the parents of  P1 so as to be found guilty. The learned trial Judge answered all the questions in the affirmative and opined that the prosecution had been able to prove the offences to the hilt and, accordingly, imposed the sentence as stated hereinbefore. Grieved by the judgment of conviction and the order of sentence the appellants filed an appeal. The High Court at the stage of admission had suo motu issued notice for enhancement of sentence. The State had appealed for the self-same purpose. The appeals and the revision application were disposed of by a common judgment dated 6.9.2007 whereby the Division Bench of the High Court concurred with the view expressed by the learned trial Judge and, accordingly, dismissed the appeals preferred by the accused as well as by the State and resultantly suo motu by the High Court also stood dismissed. The non-success in the appeal has compelled the accused-appellants to prefer this appeal by special leave.
Osporavajući pravnu prihvatljivost presude i rješenja Visokog suda, kojima je potvrđena presuda uvaženog dodatnog sudije Sesijskog suda, kojom je uvaženi sudija prvostepenog suda oglasio žalioce krivim za krivična djela i izrekao kaznu strogog zatvora od sedam godina i novčanu kaznu od Rs.1,000/- po prvoj tački, pet godina strogog zatvora i novčanu kaznu od Rs.1,000/- po drugoj tački, osamnaest mjeseci strogog zatvora i novčanu kaznu od Rs.500/- po trećoj tački i šest mjeseci strogog zatvora i novčanu kaznu od Rs.250/- po četvrtoj tački, uz odredbu o zamjenskoj kazni za slučaj neplaćanja novčane kazne za svako od krivičnih djela. Uvaženi sudija prvostepenog suda je odredio da se sve kazne izdržavaju istovremeno. Izostavljajući nebitne pojedinosti, verzija optužbe je, ukratko, da je brak između žalioca br. 1 i pokojne  P1, sestre podnosioca prijave, PW-2, zaključen 24. septembra 1997. godine. Nakon sklapanja braka pokojna je živjela sa suprugom i svekrvom (ovdje žalilac br.2) u bračnom domu. Iz braka je rođeno dvoje djece, sin i kćerka. Dana 11. septembra 2001. godine podnosilac prijave, brat pokojne, primio je telefonski poziv od optuženog br. 1 da je njegova sestra  P1 izvršila samoubistvo. Po prijemu telefonskog poziva došao je sa svojim prijateljem, P2, PW-20, i u tom trenutku suprug  P1,  P3, obavijestio je da je pokojnoj dojadilo stalno loše zdravlje njene djece i da ju je ta frustracija navela da izvrši samoubistvo vezavši 'dupatta' oko vrata. Brat pokojne nije povjerovao u verziju  P3 i podnio je krivičnu prijavu (FIR) navodeći da su suprug i svekrva pokojne, nakon sklapanja braka, neprestano tražili od oca pokojne miraz u iznosu od Rs.2 lakha, ali kako taj zahtjev nije mogao biti ispunjen zbog materijalnog stanja oca, suprug i njegova majka su počeli da je zlostavljaju u bračnom domu i, budući da nije mogla da podnese fizičko i psihičko mučenje, bila je primorana da izvrši samoubistvo. Treba napomenuti da je, budući da je smrt bila neprirodna, policija poslala tijelo na obdukciju, a ljekar koji je izvršio obdukciju dao je mišljenje da je smrt nastupila usljed samoubistva. Nakon što je krivični postupak pokrenut na osnovu krivične prijave (FIR) koju je podnio brat, službenik koji je vodio istragu saslušao je veći broj svjedoka i, nakon okončanja svih formalnosti, podnio optužni akt nadležnom sudu, koji je predmet uputio Sesijskom sudu. Optuženi su negirali navode i zahtijevali da im se sudi. Tužilaštvo je, u cilju dokazivanja optužbi podignutih protiv optuženih, saslušalo 22 svjedoka i izvelo dokaz uvidom u veći broj isprava. Odbrana je odlučila da ne izvodi dokaze. 4. Uvaženi sudija prvostepenog suda je postavio prvenstveno četiri pitanja, i to: da li su optuženi nanosili pokojnoj nepodnošljivo mučenje i psihičko uznemiravanje, tako da su odgovorni za kažnjavanje; da li materijal izveden u spisima dokazuje krivično djelo; da li je fizičko i psihičko mučenje pokojnu natjeralo da izvrši samoubistvo 11. septembra 2001. godine, usljed čega su optuženi postali odgovorni da budu osuđeni; i da li su optuženi od roditelja  P1 tražili iznos od Rs.2 lakha na ime miraza, tako da budu oglašeni krivim. Uvaženi sudija prvostepenog suda je na sva pitanja odgovorio potvrdno i zaključio da je tužilaštvo u potpunosti dokazalo krivična djela i, shodno tome, izrekao kaznu kako je prethodno navedeno. Nezadovoljni osuđujućom presudom i rješenjem o kazni, žalioci su izjavili žalbu. Visoki sud je u fazi prihvatanja žalbe suo motu izdao obavještenje radi povećanja kazne. Država je izjavila žalbu u istom cilju. O žalbama i zahtjevu za preispitivanje (reviziji) odlučeno je zajedničkom presudom od 6. septembra 2007. godine, kojom se vijeće (Division Bench) Visokog suda saglasilo sa stavom uvaženog sudije prvostepenog suda i, shodno tome, odbilo žalbe koje su izjavili optuženi i država, pa je, posljedično, odbijen i postupak koji je Visoki sud pokrenuo suo motu. Neuspjeh u žalbenom postupku primorao je optužene-žalioce da izjave ovu žalbu po posebnoj dozvoli.
queries · 00022
The petitioner is a firm carrying on business as builders, colonizers and contractors. The petitioner is the owner of 24.45 acres of vacant land situated in s village. Being engaged in construction activities it made an application under the provisions for the grant of licence for group housing scheme. This application was submitted by the petitioner on July 21, 1983 without necessary documents. After several representations the Director of Town and Country Planning Department replied that it was proposed to grant licence to the petitioner for setting up of Group Housing Colony. The petitioner was called upon to fulfil the conditions laid down in the rules within a period of 30 days from the date of service of that notice. The agreement was required to be executed on non-judicial stamp paper of Rs 3. The petitioner was also called upon to execute a bank guarantee for Rs 109.30 lakhs. On a request made by the petitioner for extension of time for furnishing the bank guarantee, time was extended (vide letter dated April 5, 1984) by four weeks. A further extension was prayed by the petitioner that was also granted on July 5, 1984 granting a further extension by four weeks. On October 19, 1984, while returning the estimates for development and service plans, respondent 2 (Director of Town and Country Planning) for 24.45 acres, the petitioner was directed to submit the estimate only for 21.15 acres. On receipt of this letter, the petitioner wrote on November 14, 1984 that the external development charges which were demanded by respondent 2 might be reduced in view of the reduction in the area. This request of the petitioner was not acceded to by the Director. The petitioner was called upon to execute an agreement under bank guarantee as already asked for. After some lapse of time, on December 12, 1987, the petitioner explained the circumstances under which he could not arrange for the bank guarantee of Rs 109.30 lakhs earlier. The bankers were willing to provide a bank guarantee for Rs 109.30 lakhs within 30 days of his intimating them to do so. It was also stated that he was willing to abide by all the directions and conditions which had been prescribed by respondent 2. Again, on September 26, 1988, October 11, 1988, January 2, 1989 and January 7, 1989, the request was rejected for the revival of the sanction and agreeing to comply with the rules and conditions. On September 25, 1989, the petitioner was informed that it is a request for grant of a licence which was refused since it had failed to fulfil the conditions laid down under Rule 11 within the stipulated/extended period. Upon receipt of this letter, the petitioner filed a memorandum to the Governor on May 4, 1991 since the State was under President's rule. It appears from the affidavit that the Governor made a suggestion that the licence granted in February 1984 could be revalidated if the petitioner was ready and willing to pay interest at bank rate on the amount of Rs 109.30 lakhs which was demanded as a security by the Director of Town and Country Planning w.e.f. 1984. Thereupon, the petitioner consented to such a course. The affidavit further avers that the Governor while recording the statement of the petitioner directed to submit a report and to calculate the total amount. 6.  With the change of the government, these directions of the Governor were not implemented. Hence, in this present petition, the petitioner prays for an order in the nature of mandamus to direct the State of Haryana to revalidate the licence/permission granted by the Director by his letter dated 'Nil' Memo No. 1823-5 DP-84 permitting the petitioner to construct the multi-storeyed houses and flats in accordance with law by accepting security/bank guarantee to the tune of Rs 109.30 lakhs as demanded by the respondent. 7.  A further prayer is for permission, approval or sanction to enable the petitioner to construct the multi-storeyed houses in pursuance of the licence granted by respondent 2 in the year 1984.
Podnosilac zahtjeva je firma koja se bavi poslovima građevinara, urbanizatora (colonizers) i izvođača radova. Podnosilac zahtjeva je vlasnik 24.45 akera neizgrađenog zemljišta koje se nalazi u selu s. Budući da se bavi građevinskom djelatnošću, podnio je zahtjev po osnovu odgovarajućih odredbi za izdavanje licence za program grupne stambene izgradnje. Ovaj zahtjev podnosilac zahtjeva je podnio 21. jula 1983. godine bez potrebne dokumentacije. Nakon više predstavki, direktor Odjeljenja za urbanizam i prostorno planiranje odgovorio je da se predlaže izdavanje licence podnosiocu zahtjeva za izgradnju naselja grupne stambene izgradnje. Podnosilac zahtjeva je pozvan da ispuni uslove propisane pravilima u roku od 30 dana od dana dostavljanja tog obavještenja. Ugovor je trebalo zaključiti na nesudskom taksenom papiru od Rs 3. Podnosilac zahtjeva je pozvan i da obezbijedi bankarsku garanciju na iznos od Rs 109.30 lakha. Po zahtjevu podnosioca zahtjeva za produženje roka za dostavljanje bankarske garancije, rok je produžen (dopisom od 5. aprila 1984. godine) za četiri sedmice. Podnosilac zahtjeva je tražio dalje produženje, koje je takođe odobreno 5. jula 1984. godine, čime je rok produžen za još četiri sedmice. Dana 19. oktobra 1984. godine, prilikom vraćanja predračuna za razvojne i servisne planove, protivna strana 2 (direktor Odjeljenja za urbanizam i prostorno planiranje) za 24.45 akera, podnosiocu zahtjeva je naloženo da dostavi predračun samo za 21.15 akera. Po prijemu ovog dopisa, podnosilac zahtjeva je 14. novembra 1984. godine napisao da bi naknade za spoljnu infrastrukturu koje je zahtijevala protivna strana 2 mogle biti smanjene s obzirom na smanjenje površine. Direktor nije udovoljio ovom zahtjevu podnosioca zahtjeva. Podnosilac zahtjeva je pozvan da zaključi ugovor uz bankarsku garanciju, kako je već traženo. Nakon izvjesnog vremena, 12. decembra 1987. godine, podnosilac zahtjeva je objasnio okolnosti zbog kojih ranije nije mogao da obezbijedi bankarsku garanciju od Rs 109.30 lakha. Banke su bile spremne da izdaju bankarsku garanciju na iznos od Rs 109.30 lakha u roku od 30 dana od njegovog obavještenja da to učine. Navedeno je i da je spreman da poštuje sve naloge i uslove koje je propisala protivna strana 2. Ponovo je 26. septembra 1988. godine, 11. oktobra 1988. godine, 2. januara 1989. godine i 7. januara 1989. godine odbijen zahtjev za obnavljanje odobrenja i pristanak na poštovanje pravila i uslova. Dana 25. septembra 1989. godine podnosilac zahtjeva je obaviješten da se radi o zahtjevu za izdavanje licence koji je odbijen jer nije ispunio uslove propisane pravilom 11 u propisanom/produženom roku. Po prijemu ovog dopisa, podnosilac zahtjeva je 4. maja 1991. godine podnio memorandum guverneru, budući da je u državi bila uvedena predsjednička uprava. Iz izjave date pod zakletvom proizlazi da je guverner predložio da se licenca izdata u februaru 1984. godine može ponovo potvrditi ako je podnosilac zahtjeva spreman i voljan da plati kamatu po bankarskoj stopi na iznos od Rs 109.30 lakha, koji je direktor Odjeljenja za urbanizam i prostorno planiranje zahtijevao kao obezbjeđenje sa dejstvom od 1984. godine. Nakon toga, podnosilac zahtjeva je pristao na takav postupak. U izjavi datoj pod zakletvom dalje se navodi da je guverner, prilikom uzimanja izjave podnosioca zahtjeva, naložio da se podnese izvještaj i izračuna ukupan iznos. 6.  Sa promjenom vlade, ovi nalozi guvernera nijesu izvršeni. Stoga, u ovom zahtjevu, podnosilac zahtjeva traži da se donese rješenje u vidu mandamusa kojim bi se državi Harijana naložilo da ponovo potvrdi licencu/odobrenje koje je izdao direktor dopisom od 'Nil' Memo br. 1823-5 DP-84, kojim se podnosiocu zahtjeva dozvoljava izgradnja višespratnih kuća i stanova u skladu sa zakonom, uz prihvatanje obezbjeđenja/bankarske garancije u iznosu od Rs 109.30 lakha, kako je zahtijevala protivna strana. 7.  Dalji zahtjev se odnosi na dozvolu, saglasnost ili odobrenje kako bi se podnosiocu zahtjeva omogućila izgradnja višespratnih kuća na osnovu licence koju je 1984. godine izdala protivna strana 2.

These appeals are directed against the judgment of a High Court whereby an appeal and a criminal revision were disposed of. The appellants were found guilty and sentenced to undergo various terms of sentences. The Criminal Appeal was filed by three appellants questioning the conviction and sentence as recorded. Complainant filed a revision petition stating that she was entitled to compensation. Background facts giving rise to the trial are essentially as follows: "The complainant and the appellants are first cousins, and as such are closely related to each other. Their grandfather was P1. As per site plans Ex. PP prepared by P2, P3 PW4 and Ex. PT prepared by P3 PW9 (I.0.), it shows that the place of occurrence was in the common land owned both by the appellants and the complainant party. The tube well of which the pipes were being taken out by the appellants, was also in the common piece of land. P4 (hereinafter referred to as 'deceased') was standing in the water-course point B (Ex.PT). Complainant P5 was standing in the common land Point C (Ex.PT) and P6 was standing at Point D (Ex. PT). It is the appellants who went 16 to 35 feet towards the complainants where deceased P4 and the other two witnesses P5 (PW6) and P6 (PW7) were standing and thereafter attacked them. P5 (PW6) asked the appellants not to take out the iron and plastic pipes of the tube well, but firstly to talk to the elders. Malkiat Singh, Patwari (PW4), who is a key witness in regard to the ownership of the piece of land where the tubewell was installed, was not put any question regarding the ownership of the common land. P5 (PW6), in his testimony before the Court, stated that the appellants on 7.1.2001 at about 1.00 P.M. armed with spades came to the tube well and started removing the pipes, which was jointly owned by both the appellants and complainant party. On being stopped, the appellants felt offended and attacked the complainant party. He (PW6) has further stated that there was no dispute regarding the joint property, but the appellants were not on visiting terms with them as far social functions were concerned. P4 was attacked in the joint water channel and across the water channel there was the field of P7, father of P8. After leaving the common pipes of land where the tube well was installed, rest of the land had been divided by both the parties and they were cultivating the land separately and peacefully. The complainant party did not have any weapons in their hands when they had gone to stop the appellants. This witness (PW6) has stated that they did not go near the appellants, but asked them not to remove the pipes. They were at that time standing at a distance of 5-6 karms. P6 (PW7) has also reiterated the same. P5 (PW6) has stated, that P9 and P10 have their fields at a distance of about half a kills from the place of occurrence. Both these witnesses P5 (PW6) and P6 (PW7) corroborate each other inter-se and also corroborate the FIR The medical evidence also corroborates the statements given by the eye witnesses. Dr. D1 (PW 1) has stated in his testimony, that on examining P5 he found that he had received one incised wound injury on the scalp left parietal area vertical in position. Similarly on examining P6, he found the first injury to be an incised wound. Second and third were abrasions on the left shoulder and neck. The fourth injury was a lacerated wound on the right parietal area of scalp. On the post-mortem conducted on P4, an incised wound was found on the parietal area of the scalp, about 12 cms from right ear pinna backwards, traversing part of left parietal area of scalp to left occipital area. The medical evidence corroborates the ocular account." Trial court took note of the fact that the appellants and the members of the complainant party are related to each other closely. The dispute arose because of conflicting claims as to the ownership of the land. It was submitted that the occurrence took place when the members of the complainant party came forward and obstructed the appellant from doing the work and restrained them from pulling out the pipe. There was exchange of hot words and in the process, the occurrence, according to the prosecution, took place. In essence it was submitted that the accused were exercising the right of private defence or in the alternative the occurrence took place in the course of a sudden quarrel. Stand of the State was that there appears to be some exchange of words. The trial court found substance in the plea and found the accused persons guilty. Before the High Court it was submitted that the factual scenario has not been correctly appreciated by the trial court. It noted that the appellants pulled out the iron and plastic pipes which were installed on the land jointly owned by both the parties. Since the accused persons pulled out the pipes it was natural that the members of the complainant party who were standing at a distance of 16 to 35 feets from the appellants intervened and asked them not to pull out the pipes unless the elders take a decision. The appellants did not pay any heed.
Ove žalbe su izjavljene protiv presude Visokog suda kojom je odlučeno o žalbi i o krivičnom zahtjevu za preispitivanje (reviziji). Žalioci su oglašeni krivim i osuđeni na kazne različitog trajanja. Krivičnu žalbu su izjavila tri žalioca, osporavajući osuđujuću presudu i izrečenu kaznu. Podnositeljka prijave je podnijela reviziju, navodeći da ima pravo na naknadu. Činjenice koje su dovele do suđenja su u suštini sljedeće: "Podnosilac prijave i žalioci su prvi rođaci, i kao takvi su u bliskom srodstvu. Njihov djed je bio P1. Prema skicama lica mjesta Ex. PP, koju je izradio P2, P3 PW4, i Ex. PT, koju je izradio P3 PW9 (I.0.), proizilazi da se mjesto događaja nalazilo na zajedničkom zemljištu u svojini kako žalilaca, tako i strane podnosioca prijave. Bušeni bunar iz kojeg su žalioci vadili cijevi takođe se nalazio na zajedničkoj parceli zemljišta. P4 (u daljem tekstu: 'pokojni') stajao je u vodotoku, na tački B (Ex.PT). Podnosilac prijave P5 stajao je na zajedničkom zemljištu, na tački C (Ex.PT), a P6 je stajao na tački D (Ex. PT). Upravo su žalioci prešli 16 do 35 stopa prema podnosiocima prijave, gdje su stajali pokojni P4 i druga dva svjedoka, P5 (PW6) i P6 (PW7), i nakon toga ih napali. P5 (PW6) je zamolio žalioce da ne vade gvozdene i plastične cijevi iz bušenog bunara, već da najprije razgovaraju sa starješinama. Malkiat Singh, Patwari (PW4), koji je ključni svjedok u pogledu svojine na parceli zemljišta na kojoj je bio postavljen bušeni bunar, nije mu postavljeno nijedno pitanje u vezi sa svojinom na zajedničkom zemljištu. P5 (PW6) je u svom iskazu pred sudom naveo da su žalioci 7. januara 2001. godine, oko 1.00 čas poslije podne, naoružani lopatama, došli do bušenog bunara, koji je bio u zajedničkoj svojini i žalilaca i strane podnosioca prijave, i počeli da uklanjaju cijevi. Kada su zaustavljeni, žalioci su se osjetili uvrijeđenim i napali stranu podnosioca prijave. On (PW6) je dalje naveo da nije bilo spora u pogledu zajedničke imovine, ali da žalioci nijesu sa njima održavali odnose kada su u pitanju društvena okupljanja. P4 je napadnut u zajedničkom vodenom kanalu, a s druge strane vodenog kanala nalazila se njiva P7, oca P8. Izuzev zajedničke parcele zemljišta na kojoj je bio postavljen bušeni bunar, ostatak zemljišta su obje strane podijelile i obrađivale su zemljište odvojeno i mirno. Strana podnosioca prijave nije imala nikakvo oružje u rukama kada je otišla da zaustavi žalioce. Ovaj svjedok (PW6) je naveo da nijesu prilazili žaliocima, već da su ih zamolili da ne uklanjaju cijevi. Oni su u to vrijeme stajali na udaljenosti od 5-6 karma. P6 (PW7) je takođe ponovio isto. P5 (PW6) je naveo da P9 i P10 imaju svoje njive na udaljenosti od oko pola killa od mjesta događaja. Oba ova svjedoka, P5 (PW6) i P6 (PW7), međusobno potvrđuju iskaze jedan drugog (inter-se) i takođe potvrđuju krivičnu prijavu (FIR) Medicinski dokazi takođe potvrđuju izjave koje su dali očevici. Dr D1 (PW 1) je u svom iskazu naveo da je pregledom P5 utvrdio da je on zadobio jednu sječenu ranu na vlasištu, u lijevoj tjemenoj regiji, u vertikalnom položaju. Isto tako, pregledom P6 utvrdio je da je prva povreda sječena rana. Druga i treća povreda bile su ogrebotine na lijevom ramenu i vratu. Četvrta povreda bila je razderotina u desnoj tjemenoj regiji vlasišta. Prilikom obdukcije izvršene na P4 utvrđena je sječena rana u tjemenoj regiji vlasišta, oko 12 cm unazad od ušne školjke desnog uha, koja je prolazila preko dijela lijeve tjemene regije vlasišta do lijeve potiljačne regije. Medicinski dokazi potvrđuju iskaze očevidaca." Prvostepeni sud je uzeo u obzir činjenicu da su žalioci i članovi strane podnosioca prijave u bliskom srodstvu. Spor je nastao zbog suprotstavljenih zahtjeva u pogledu svojine na zemljištu. Isticano je da se događaj odigrao kada su članovi strane podnosioca prijave istupili i ometali žalioca u obavljanju posla i spriječili ih da izvade cijev. Došlo je do žestoke prepirke i pri tome se, prema navodima optužbe, odigrao događaj. U suštini je isticano da su optuženi vršili pravo na nužnu odbranu ili, alternativno, da se događaj odigrao u toku iznenadne svađe. Stav države je bio da je, kako izgleda, došlo do izvjesne razmjene riječi. Prvostepeni sud je našao da je taj navod osnovan i oglasio optužena lica krivim. Pred Visokim sudom je isticano da prvostepeni sud nije pravilno ocijenio činjenično stanje. Visoki sud je konstatovao da su žalioci izvadili gvozdene i plastične cijevi koje su bile postavljene na zemljištu u zajedničkoj svojini obje strane. Budući da su optužena lica izvadila cijevi, bilo je prirodno da članovi strane podnosioca prijave, koji su stajali na udaljenosti od 16 do 35 stopa od žalilaca, intervenišu i zamole ih da ne vade cijevi dok starješine ne donesu odluku. Žalioci se na to nijesu obazirali.
queries · 00024
These appeals involving common questions of law and fact were taken up for hearing together and are being disposed of by this common judgment.  The lands situated inter alia in villages V1, V2, V3 and V4 were acquired by the State for the purpose of use thereof by the Appellant. A notification was issued on 31.7.1986. The declaration was issued on 29.12.1987. Upon service of notice upon the claimants, the Collector made an award. In doing so, several deeds of sale executed between 1981 and 1982 in respect of lands adjoining some of the villages were taken into consideration and market value of the land was determined at the rate of Rs. 1.55 per sq. m. The claimants Respondents did not accept the said award and prayed for a reference to the Civil Court. Such a reference having been made the Reference Court purported to be relying on or on the basis of judgments dated 30th October, 1996 and 10th November, 1996 passed by 4th Extra Assistant Judge and 2nd Extra Assistant Judge respectively in L.A. R. Case No. 1349/92 and 1314/92 passed an award computing the amount of compensation at the rate of Rs. 10/- per sq. m. The Appellant herein was not impleaded as a party in the Reference Court. It had, thus, no opportunity also to adduce any evidence either before the Land Acquisition Collector or before the Reference Court. It preferred appeals before the High Court being aggrieved by and dissatisfied with the said judgment and award passed by the Reference Court. A contention raised by the Appellant before the High Court inter alia was that the Reference Judge acted illegally and without jurisdiction in passing the said judgment solely on the basis of the deposition of one P1 who alleged that the agricultural lands which he and others had been cultivating were of high fertility and three crops in a year were grown therein. The witness further alleged that the village was well-developed. He further contended that the lands of one P2 was acquired for the Appellant wherein compensation at the rate of Rs. 10/- per sq. m. was awarded. It was argued that the Reference Court was bound to consider the deeds of sale relied upon by the Collector in his Award. High Court rejected the said contentions stating that the Reference Court had not committed any error of law in taking into consideration the evidence adduced by the said witness. It was held: "It appears that after the evidence, another judgment was pointed to the Reference Court for which there is a reference in the impugned judgment. The lands covered under that reference cases were situated in the sim of village V1 and V5 and the Reference Court awarded Rs. 10/- per sq. mtrs. In the instant case, the lands are situated at village V1. In view of this evidence, we find no substance in the appeals and appeals are dismissed."
Ove žalbe, koje se odnose na zajednička pravna i činjenična pitanja, uzete su zajedno u razmatranje i o njima se odlučuje ovom zajedničkom presudom.  Zemljišta koja se nalaze, između ostalog, u selima V1, V2, V3 i V4 eksproprisala je država radi njihovog korišćenja od strane žalioca. Obavještenje je izdato 31. jula 1986. godine. Izjava je izdata 29. decembra 1987. godine. Nakon dostavljanja poziva podnosiocima zahtjeva za naknadu, kolektor (Collector) je donio odluku o naknadi. Pri tome je uzeto u obzir više kupoprodajnih ugovora zaključenih između 1981. i 1982. godine u vezi sa zemljištima koja se graniče sa nekim od sela, a tržišna vrijednost zemljišta utvrđena je po stopi od Rs. 1.55 po kv. m. Podnosioci zahtjeva za naknadu, protivne strane, nijesu prihvatili navedenu odluku o naknadi i zatražili su upućivanje predmeta građanskom sudu. Pošto je predmet upućen, Referentni sud je, navodno oslanjajući se na presude od 30. oktobra 1996. godine i 10. novembra 1996. godine, koje su donijeli 4. dodatni pomoćni sudija, odnosno 2. dodatni pomoćni sudija u predmetima L.A. R. br. 1349/92 i 1314/92, ili na osnovu tih presuda, donio odluku o naknadi kojom je iznos naknade obračunat po stopi od Rs. 10/- po kv. m. Žalilac u ovom postupku nije bio uključen kao strana pred Referentnim sudom. On, dakle, nije imao ni mogućnost da izvede bilo kakve dokaze, bilo pred kolektorom za eksproprijaciju zemljišta, bilo pred Referentnim sudom. Nezadovoljan navedenom presudom i odlukom o naknadi koje je donio Referentni sud, izjavio je žalbe Visokom sudu. Žalilac je pred Visokim sudom, između ostalog, isticao da je sudija Referentnog suda postupio nezakonito i bez nadležnosti kada je donio navedenu presudu isključivo na osnovu iskaza izvjesnog P1, koji je tvrdio da je poljoprivredno zemljište koje su on i drugi obrađivali bilo veoma plodno i da su se na njemu uzgajala tri usjeva godišnje. Svjedok je dalje tvrdio da je selo bilo dobro razvijeno. On je dalje tvrdio da je zemljište izvjesnog P2 eksproprisano za potrebe žalioca, pri čemu je dosuđena naknada po stopi od Rs. 10/- po kv. m. Isticano je da je Referentni sud bio dužan da razmotri kupoprodajne ugovore na koje se kolektor oslonio u svojoj odluci o naknadi. Visoki sud je odbio navedene navode, uz obrazloženje da Referentni sud nije počinio nikakvu pravnu grešku time što je uzeo u obzir dokaze koje je izveo navedeni svjedok. Zauzet je stav: "Proizilazi da je nakon izvođenja dokaza Referentnom sudu ukazano na još jednu presudu, koja se pominje u pobijanoj presudi. Zemljišta obuhvaćena tim predmetima upućivanja nalazila su se u ataru sela V1 i V5, a Referentni sud je dosudio Rs. 10/- po kv. m. U ovom predmetu, zemljišta se nalaze u selu V1. S obzirom na ove dokaze, nalazimo da žalbe nijesu osnovane i žalbe se odbijaju."
