Appellants call in question legality of the judgment rendered by the High Court upholding conviction of the appellants (hereinafter referred to as the 'accused') and sentence as imposed by the trial Court which had sentenced each to undergo rigorous imprisonment for three months, two years and seven years respectively with separate fines for each of the alleged offences with default stipulations. Background facts leading to the trial of the accused appellants are as follows: The case was registered on the basis of information lodged by P1 (PW-6), which was recorded on 10.11.1989 at about 2.00 a.m. According to the informant, he and his son P2's wife P3 (PW- 7) were sitting in the courtyard of the house of P2 (hereinafter referred to as the 'deceased'). It was about 11.00 a.m. on 9.11.1989 when deceased was coming from the village after purchasing vegetables. When he reached near the house of P4, son of P5, P6 (A-1) armed with a Gandasi and P7 (A-2) armed with a lathi were present there. P7 made an obscene gesture. At this P7 and the deceased exchanged hot words and abused each other. P6 gave a Gandasi blow on the right hand of the deceased, which caused a grievous injury. P7 gave a lathi blow on the left foot of the deceased and also gave a thrust blow of lathi on the left side of his head. Deceased fell down on the ground. The occurrence was witnessed by P1 (PW-6) and P3 (PW-7). Both of them took P2 injured to their house. When P1 and P3 raised alarm, both the accused persons ran away from the spot. Since the condition of P2 became serious during the night time, he was taken to the Primary Health Centre. On 10.11.1989 at night at about 0.15 a.m. Dr.D1 examined P2. He found injury No.1 which was an incised wound in the middle phalanx of the index finger of right hand. The second injury was an abrasion on the lateral side of upper 1/3rd part of left leg and the patient complained of pain on different parts of the body. Dr.D1 sent information to the Police Station. ASI P7 reached the Primary Health Centre and recorded the statement of P1 which is the FIR. On the basis of this statement, FIR was recorded by ASI P8. P2 expired at about 1.50 a.m. on 10.11.1989. Hence, information to this effect was sent. The accused persons were charged for offences. The accused persons pleaded innocence and claimed trial. Eight persons were examined to further the prosecution case. P1 (PW-6) was the complainant and claimed to be an eye-witness. P3 (PW-7) wife of the deceased also claimed to be an eye-witness. Placing reliance on their evidence, the learned Additional Session Judge found the accused persons guilty and sentenced them to undergo sentences as noted above. In appeal, the High Court confirmed the conviction and the sentences. In support of the appeal, learned counsel for the accused appellants submitted that the prosecution version was based on testimonies of relatives and, therefore, does not inspire confidence. Further there was delay in lodging the FIR. Additionally, it was submitted that the sentences imposed were high.
Žalioci osporavaju zakonitost presude Visokog suda kojom je potvrđena osuđujuća presuda žaliocima (u daljem tekstu: optuženi) i kazna koju je izrekao prvostepeni sud, koji je svakog od njih osudio na strogi zatvor u trajanju od tri mjeseca, dvije godine, odnosno sedam godina, uz posebne novčane kazne za svako od krivičnih djela koja im se stavljaju na teret, sa odredbama o zamjenskoj kazni u slučaju neplaćanja. Činjenično stanje koje je dovelo do suđenja optuženim žaliocima je sljedeće: Predmet je evidentiran na osnovu prijave koju je podnio P1 (PW-6), a koja je zapisnički primljena 10. novembra 1989. godine oko 2.00 časa. Prema navodima podnosioca prijave, on i supruga njegovog sina P2, P3 (PW- 7), sjedjeli su u dvorištu kuće P2 (u daljem tekstu: pokojni). Bilo je oko 11.00 časova 9. novembra 1989. godine kada se pokojni vraćao iz sela nakon kupovine povrća. Kada je stigao blizu kuće P4, sina P5, tamo su se nalazili P6 (A-1), naoružan gandasom (Gandasi), i P7 (A-2), naoružan motkom (lathi). P7 je napravio vulgaran gest. Na to su P7 i pokojni razmijenili oštre riječi i izvrijeđali jedan drugog. P6 je zadao udarac gandasom po desnoj ruci pokojnog, čime mu je nanio tešku tjelesnu povredu. P7 je zadao udarac motkom po lijevom stopalu pokojnog, a zadao mu je i udarac vrhom motke u lijevu stranu glave. Pokojni je pao na zemlju. Događaj su vidjeli P1 (PW-6) i P3 (PW-7). Oboje su povrijeđenog P2 odnijeli u svoju kuću. Kada su P1 i P3 pozvali u pomoć, oba optužena su pobjegla sa lica mjesta. Budući da se stanje P2 tokom noći pogoršalo, odvezen je u Dom zdravlja. Dana 10. novembra 1989. godine noću, oko 0.15 časova, dr D1 je pregledao P2. Utvrdio je povredu br.1, koja je bila sjekotina na srednjem članku kažiprsta desne ruke. Druga povreda je bila ogrebotina na bočnoj strani gornje 1/3 lijeve potkoljenice, a pacijent se žalio na bolove u različitim djelovima tijela. Dr D1 je o tome obavijestio policijsku stanicu. Pomoćnik podinspektora (ASI) P7 je došao u Dom zdravlja i zapisnički uzeo izjavu P1, koja predstavlja krivičnu prijavu (FIR). Na osnovu te izjave, pomoćnik podinspektora (ASI) P8 je evidentirao krivičnu prijavu (FIR). P2 je preminuo oko 1.50 časova 10. novembra 1989. godine. Stoga je o tome poslato obavještenje. Optuženima je stavljeno na teret izvršenje krivičnih djela. Optuženi su izjavili da nijesu krivi i zahtijevali su suđenje. Radi dokazivanja navoda optužbe saslušano je osam lica. P1 (PW-6) je bio podnosilac prijave i tvrdio je da je očevidac. P3 (PW-7), supruga pokojnog, takođe je tvrdila da je očevidac. Oslanjajući se na njihove iskaze, uvaženi dodatni sudija Sesijskog suda oglasio je optužene krivim i izrekao im kazne kako je prethodno navedeno. U žalbenom postupku Visoki sud je potvrdio osuđujuću presudu i kazne. U prilog žalbi, uvaženi zastupnik optuženih žalilaca istakao je da se verzija optužbe zasniva na iskazima srodnika i da stoga ne uliva povjerenje. Dalje, došlo je do kašnjenja u podnošenju krivične prijave (FIR). Pored toga, istaknuto je da su izrečene kazne visoke.

The appellant herein is a Senior Manager in a fertilizer company C1. The said company is engaged in manufacturing and selling of fertilizer. The Government of India in exercise of power has framed an order known as the Fertilizer (Control) Order, 1985 for controlling the quality of fertilizer. It appears that the samples of fertilizer sold by the appellant was taken from stitched bags of fertilizer and thereafter sent to laboratory for analysis. It is alleged that the laboratory reported that the fertilizer is sub-standard. It is further alleged that on the strength of the said report the respondent threatened the appellant to prosecute him. It is under such circumstances the appellant filed a petition before the High Court challenging the validity of Clause 19 of the Fertilizer (Control) Order. The case of the appellant was that since Clause 19 does not permit an accused a right to adduce evidence to contradict the report of the public analyst, the said clause is ultra vires articles of the Constitution. The said contention of the appellant was accepted by a Single Judge of the High Court. Consequently, Clause 19 of the Fertilizer (Control) Order was struck down. Aggrieved by the judgment of the learned Single Judge, the respondents preferred a letters patent appeal before the Division Bench of the High Court. The Letters Patent Bench, allowed the appeal and set aside the judgment of the learned Single Judge. It is against the said judgment of the High Court, the appellant is in appeal before us. It is not disputed that no charge-sheet on the strength of public analyst's report has been submitted to the Court against the appellant.
Žalilac u ovom postupku je viši menadžer u kompaniji za vještačko đubrivo C1. Navedena kompanija se bavi proizvodnjom i prodajom vještačkog đubriva. Vlada Indije je, u vršenju ovlašćenja, donijela naredbu pod nazivom Naredba o kontroli vještačkog đubriva iz 1985. godine (Fertilizer (Control) Order) radi kontrole kvaliteta vještačkog đubriva. Proizilazi da su uzorci vještačkog đubriva koje je prodavao žalilac uzeti iz zašivenih vreća vještačkog đubriva i potom poslati u laboratoriju na analizu. Navodi se da je laboratorija izvijestila da je vještačko đubrivo ispod standarda. Dalje se navodi da je, na osnovu tog izvještaja, protivna strana zaprijetila žaliocu krivičnim gonjenjem. U takvim okolnostima žalilac je Visokom sudu podnio zahtjev kojim je osporio valjanost tačke 19 Naredbe o kontroli vještačkog đubriva. Žalilac je tvrdio da je, budući da tačka 19 ne daje okrivljenom pravo da izvodi dokaze kojima bi osporio izvještaj javnog analitičara, navedena tačka ultra vires u odnosu na članove Ustava. Taj navod žalioca prihvatio je sudija pojedinac Visokog suda. Shodno tome, tačka 19 Naredbe o kontroli vještačkog đubriva stavljena je van snage. Nezadovoljne presudom uvaženog sudije pojedinca, protivne strane su izjavile žalbu po osnovu letters patent vijeću (Division Bench) Visokog suda. Vijeće po osnovu letters patent usvojilo je žalbu i stavilo van snage presudu uvaženog sudije pojedinca. Protiv te presude Visokog suda žalilac je izjavio žalbu ovom sudu. Nije sporno da sudu nije podnijet optužni akt protiv žalioca na osnovu izvještaja javnog analitičara.

Druga izjava koja sadrži priznanje, Ex.P-12 od 22. septembra 2003. godine, data pred P.W.42, Subbiah i P.W.24, dovela je do pronalaska privjeska "M" (dollar) (M.0.38) i privjeska za ključeve sa privjeskom za ključeve (M.0.39) iz toaleta u sobi optuženog. Optužba se oslonila i na navodni motiv, u smislu da je optuženom hitno bio potreban novac kako bi poboljšao ocjene iz matematike, te da je stoga optuženi ukrao predmete koji su pripadali pokojnoj. Prvostepeni sud je oglasio optuženog krivim, donio osuđujuću presudu i izrekao kaznu kako je prethodno navedeno.  Visoki sud je utvrdio da istaknute okolnosti nijesu bile dovoljne da se krivica pripiše optuženom i naložio je oslobađanje. Uvaženi zastupnik žalioca je istakao da Visoki sud nije uzeo u obzir da istaknute okolnosti jasno uspostavljaju lanac okolnosti koji potvrđuje verziju optužbe, te da Visoki sud nije imao opravdanja da naloži oslobađanje. Uvaženi zastupnik protivne strane je, s druge strane, podržao presudu Visokog suda.

Having been selected by the Public Service Commission, the respondent herein was appointed as Law Officer-cum-Draftsman in the Directorate of Cooperation. There was only one post in the same Cadre and it had no promotional avenues. He filed a representation that his post be upgraded or two promotional avenues be provided to him. Several representations made by him having not received consideration at the hands of the appellants, the respondent herein filed a writ petition seeking for a specific direction upon the appellant herein to provide at least two promotional avenues. The said contention of the respondent was accepted by the High Court and by reason of its impugned judgment the appellant was directed to provide 'the graded scale' to the appellant by providing three grades, the initial being Grade III which is the Post of Law Officer cum Draftsman and thereafter Grade II and Grade I. Officer of Judicial Service. It was further directed: "The scale of pay of Grade II Law officer-cum- Draftsman shall be same as Grade-II officer of the Judicial Service. The scale of pay of Grade-I Law Officer-cum-Draftsman shall be equal to the scale of pay of Grade-I officer of Judicial Service." Questioning the said direction, the appellants are before us. The learned counsel appearing on behalf of the appellant would submit that the High Court went wrong in issuing the aforementioned direction. The learned counsel would urge that the respondent herein did not have any legal right to be promoted to a higher post far less the right to get the scale of pay of Grade I officer of the Judicial Service. Such a direction by the High Court, the learned counsel would contend, is wholly without jurisdiction. The learned counsel, appearing on behalf of the respondent, however, has supported the said order.
Protivna strana u ovom predmetu, koju je izabrala Komisija za javne službe, postavljena je za pravnog službenika i izrađivača propisa u Direkciji za zadrugarstvo. U istom kadru postojalo je samo jedno radno mjesto i ono nije imalo mogućnosti napredovanja. Protivna strana je podnijela predstavku da se njeno radno mjesto unaprijedi ili da joj se obezbijede dvije mogućnosti napredovanja. Budući da žalioci nijesu razmotrili više predstavki koje je podnijela, protivna strana u ovom predmetu podnijela je ustavnu tužbu (writ petition) tražeći da se žaliocu u ovom predmetu izda izričit nalog da obezbijedi najmanje dvije mogućnosti napredovanja. Visoki sud je prihvatio navedenu tvrdnju protivne strane i osporenom presudom naložio žaliocu da žaliocu obezbijedi „razvrstanu platnu skalu“ uvođenjem tri razreda, od kojih je početni III razred, koji predstavlja radno mjesto pravnog službenika i izrađivača propisa, a zatim II razred i I razred. Službenik pravosudne službe. Dalje je naloženo: „Platna skala pravnog službenika i izrađivača propisa II razreda biće ista kao platna skala službenika II razreda pravosudne službe. Platna skala pravnog službenika i izrađivača propisa I razreda biće jednaka platnoj skali službenika I razreda pravosudne službe.“ Osporavajući navedeni nalog, žalioci su se obratili ovom sudu. Uvaženi zastupnik žalioca je naveo da je Visoki sud pogriješio kada je izdao prethodno navedeni nalog. Uvaženi zastupnik je isticao da protivna strana u ovom predmetu nije imala nikakvo zakonsko pravo da bude unaprijeđena na više radno mjesto, a još manje pravo da dobije platnu skalu službenika I razreda pravosudne službe. Takav nalog Visokog suda, kako je tvrdio uvaženi zastupnik, izdat je potpuno bez nadležnosti. Uvaženi zastupnik koji je postupao u ime protivne strane je, međutim, podržao navedeno rješenje.
queries · 00040
Appellant calls in question legality of the judgment rendered by High Court confirming his conviction for offence and sentence of imprisonment for life as awarded by the learned Sessions Judge. Background facts as unfolded during trial by the prosecution are essentially as follows. One P1 (hereinafter referred to as the 'deceased') was having industry and he employed a number of girls. The accused used to make fun of the girls/workers outside the factory and this was objected to by the deceased several times. On that score, there had been enmity between the deceased and the accused. At about 8.30 p.m. on 1.5.1990, PW-1, PW-2 and one P2 were standing in front of L1 ground, south of Mail Road. The accused was sitting on the eastern side of a culvert. There was a tube light burning and hence there was enough light at that place. At that time, the deceased, who came in a bicycle proceeding from east to west, took a turn towards south. The accused rushed to the deceased saying "you die, old man" and hit him with a stick (M.O.1) on his head. The deceased sustained injuries and there was profuse bleeding. PW-1, PW-2 and P2 immediately went near him and when the accused saw them coming near ran towards west, leaving the weapon viz., M.O.1 stick. Thereafter, PW-1, PW-2, P2 and the wife of the deceased took the deceased to the Government Hospital. After giving first aid to the deceased, the doctors in the said hospital advised to take the deceased to L2 for further treatment. The aforementioned persons thereafter took the deceased to L2 and at the Government Hospital, the deceased was treated by Doctor D1 (PW-6). The doctor found several injuries. PW-1 narrated the incident to the Head Constable (PW-10) at the police station who recorded the first information report (Ex.P-11). Same was dispatched to the Court of Judicial Magistrate. Assistant Surgeon, Government Hospital (PW-7) treated the deceased who breathed his last at about 1.25 a.m. on 2.5.1990. On receiving information about the death information was sent to the Court of Judicial Magistrate. On postmortem 6 injuries were noticed, out of which 3 were external and the rest were internal. Injuries 1 and 2 as noticed were abrasions but the fatal injury i.e. injury No.3 was stated to be 4" linear oblique sutured wound over the right parietal scalp. The doctor opined that the injury was sufficient in ordinary course of nature to cause death. On 4.5.1990 the accused was arrested and after completion of investigation the charge sheet was placed. The accused pleaded innocence.The Trial Court found that the evidence of eye witnesses PWs. 1, 2 and 3 were cogent and credible. The accused used to tease girls working in the factory of the deceased. When the deceased objected to the same, there was some misunderstanding and at the time of occurrence when the deceased was coming by bicycle, the accused rushed towards him and attacked him; resulting the fatal injury. When the eye witnesses rushed to help the deceased, the accused ran away. Placing reliance on the evidence and considering the entire material on record the trial Court found the accused guilty and convicted as aforesaid. An appeal was preferred before the High Court questioning the conviction and sentence. Before the High Court, it was urged that PWs. 1 and 2 were related to the deceased, and PW-3 was a chance witness and no credence should be put on their evidence. The High Court did not accept the plea and finding the analysis of evidence by the trial Court to be in order, upheld the conviction and sentence.
Žalilac osporava zakonitost presude Visokog suda kojom je potvrđena osuđujuća presuda za krivično djelo i kazna doživotnog zatvora koju mu je izrekao uvaženi sudija Sesijskog suda. Činjenično stanje, kako ga je tužilaštvo izložilo tokom suđenja, u suštini je sljedeće. Izvjesni P1 (u daljem tekstu: „pokojni“) imao je pogon i u njemu je zapošljavao više djevojaka. Optuženi je ispred fabrike ismijavao djevojke/radnice, čemu se pokojni više puta usprotivio. Po tom osnovu je između pokojnog i optuženog postojalo neprijateljstvo. Oko 8.30 časova uveče, 1. maja 1990. godine, PW-1, PW-2 i izvjesni P2 stajali su ispred igrališta L1, južno od Mail Road. Optuženi je sjedio na istočnoj strani propusta. Tamo je gorjela neonska svjetiljka, pa je na tom mjestu bilo dovoljno svjetla. U to vrijeme pokojni, koji je dolazio biciklom krećući se od istoka prema zapadu, skrenuo je prema jugu. Optuženi je pritrčao pokojnom govoreći „umri, starče“ i udario ga štapom (M.O.1) po glavi. Pokojni je zadobio povrede i obilno je krvario. PW-1, PW-2 i P2 su odmah prišli do njega, a kada ih je optuženi vidio kako prilaze, pobjegao je prema zapadu, ostavivši oruđe, odnosno štap M.O.1. Nakon toga su PW-1, PW-2, P2 i supruga pokojnog odvezli pokojnog u Državnu bolnicu. Nakon što su pokojnom pružili prvu pomoć, ljekari u navedenoj bolnici savjetovali su da se pokojni prebaci u L2 radi daljeg liječenja. Navedena lica su potom odvezla pokojnog u L2, gdje je u Državnoj bolnici pokojnog liječio doktor D1 (PW-6). Doktor je utvrdio više povreda. PW-1 je opisao događaj glavnom policajcu (Head Constable) (PW-10) u policijskoj stanici, koji je sačinio krivičnu prijavu (FIR) (Ex.P-11). Ona je dostavljena sudu sudskog magistrata. Pomoćnik hirurga u Državnoj bolnici (PW-7) liječio je pokojnog, koji je preminuo oko 1.25 časova poslije ponoći, 2. maja 1990. godine. Po prijemu obavještenja o smrti, obavještenje je upućeno sudu sudskog magistrata. Prilikom obdukcije utvrđeno je 6 povreda, od kojih su 3 bile spoljašnje, a ostale unutrašnje. Povrede 1 i 2, kako je utvrđeno, bile su ogrebotine, ali je za smrtonosnu povredu, odnosno povredu br. 3, navedeno da je riječ o linearnoj kosoj ušivenoj rani dužine 4 inča na desnoj tjemenoj strani poglavine. Doktor je dao mišljenje da je povreda bila dovoljna da u redovnom toku stvari prouzrokuje smrt. Optuženi je 4. maja 1990. godine uhapšen i nakon okončanja istrage podnijet je optužni akt. Optuženi je tvrdio da je nevin. Prvostepeni sud je utvrdio da su iskazi očevidaca PWs. 1, 2 i 3 bili uvjerljivi i vjerodostojni. Optuženi je zadirkivao djevojke koje su radile u fabrici pokojnog. Kada se pokojni tome usprotivio, došlo je do izvjesnog nesporazuma, a u vrijeme događaja, kada je pokojni dolazio biciklom, optuženi je pritrčao prema njemu i napao ga, što je dovelo do smrtonosne povrede. Kada su očevici pritrčali da pomognu pokojnom, optuženi je pobjegao. Oslanjajući se na dokaze i cijeneći cjelokupni materijal u spisima, prvostepeni sud je optuženog oglasio krivim i osudio ga kako je prethodno navedeno. Protiv osuđujuće presude i izrečene kazne izjavljena je žalba Visokom sudu. Pred Visokim sudom isticano je da su PWs. 1 i 2 srodnici pokojnog, a da je PW-3 slučajni svjedok, te da njihovim iskazima ne treba pokloniti vjeru. Visoki sud nije prihvatio taj navod i, nalazeći da je ocjena dokaza od strane prvostepenog suda pravilna, potvrdio je osuđujuću presudu i izrečenu kaznu.

queries · 00043
This petition is by the State directed against the order dated 10.11.1998 passed by the High Court by which the charges framed against the respondent were quashed. The relevant and necessary facts to dispose of this petition are: The respondent was working as a Road Transport Inspector in the Regional Office of the Road Transport Corporation, Bhopal and is a public servant as such. A complaint for the check period 25.9.1982 to 27.3.1993 was filed stating that he had acquired the property in excess of the known source of his income. During the investigation properties and assets belonging to his mother-in- law, father, brother and nephew were shown as assets of the respondent. The assets of his wife, who is an income-tax payer and a self earning member, were also connected with the assets of the respondent. While submitting charge sheet several important documents, which were collected during the course of investigation, were withheld. According to the respondent the said documents supported him. If those documents were considered even prima facie there was no scope to frame charges against him. At the time of framing charges the respondent made an application seeking production of these documents in court before proceeding to frame charge. But the said application was rejected stating that for the purpose of framing charges only the documents forwarded to the court need to be considered. Hence he filed Criminal Revision in the High court. The said Revision Petition was disposed of by the order dated 8.9.1997 in the following terms: - "In the result the revision is allowed, the order impugned is set-aside and it is directed that the documents made available by the accused during investigation be produced and may be taken into consideration by the court below while framing the charge." Thereafter the trial court framed charges. Aggrieved by the order dated 4.4.1988 framing charges in the Special Case No. 26/96 by the Special Judge, the respondent filed Criminal Revision. The High Court by order dated 10.11.1998 accepted the case of the respondent, set aside the order of the learned Special Judge, framing charges and discharged the respondent. In these circumstances the State has come up in this petition challenging the said order of the High Court.
Ovaj zahtjev je podnijela Država protiv rješenja od 10. novembra 1998. godine koje je donio Visoki sud, kojim su ukinute optužbe formulisane protiv protivne strane. Relevantne i neophodne činjenice za odlučivanje o ovom zahtjevu su sljedeće: Protivna strana je radila kao inspektor drumskog saobraćaja u Regionalnoj kancelariji Korporacije za drumski saobraćaj, Bhopal, i kao takva je službeno lice. Podnijeta je prijava za kontrolni period od 25. septembra 1982. godine do 27. marta 1993. godine, u kojoj je navedeno da je stekao imovinu koja prevazilazi poznate izvore njegovih prihoda. Tokom istrage, imovina i sredstva koji pripadaju njegovoj tašti, ocu, bratu i sinovcu prikazani su kao imovina protivne strane. Sa imovinom protivne strane povezana je i imovina njegove supruge, koja je obveznik poreza na dohodak i samostalno ostvaruje prihode. Prilikom podnošenja optužnog akta zadržano je više važnih isprava koje su prikupljene tokom istrage. Prema navodima protivne strane, navedene isprave su išle u njegovu korist. Da su te isprave bile razmotrene, čak ni prima facie ne bi bilo osnova za formulisanje optužbi protiv njega. U vrijeme formulisanja optužbi protivna strana je podnijela zahtjev da se te isprave podnesu sudu prije nego što se pristupi formulisanju optužbe. Međutim, navedeni zahtjev je odbijen uz obrazloženje da je za potrebe formulisanja optužbi potrebno razmotriti samo isprave koje su dostavljene sudu. Stoga je podnio krivični zahtjev za preispitivanje (reviziju) Visokom sudu. O navedenoj reviziji odlučeno je rješenjem od 8. septembra 1997. godine, kako slijedi: - „Shodno tome, revizija se usvaja, osporeno rješenje se stavlja van snage i nalaže se da se isprave koje je okrivljeni stavio na raspolaganje tokom istrage podnesu i da ih niži sud može uzeti u obzir prilikom formulisanja optužbe.“ Nakon toga je prvostepeni sud formulisao optužbe. Nezadovoljna rješenjem od 4. aprila 1988. godine o formulisanju optužbi u Posebnom predmetu br. 26/96, koje je donio posebni sudija, protivna strana je podnijela krivičnu reviziju. Visoki sud je rješenjem od 10. novembra 1998. godine prihvatio navode protivne strane, stavio van snage rješenje uvaženog posebnog sudije o formulisanju optužbi i obustavio postupak protiv protivne strane. U tim okolnostima Država je podnijela ovaj zahtjev, osporavajući navedeno rješenje Visokog suda.

The appellants were tried for offences on the allegations that about four months prior to the incident, some quarrel had taken place between the deceased P1 and the appellant No. 4 P2 in relation to raising of boundary wall. On 12 April 1983 at about 7.00 P.M. the deceased accompanied by P3, had gone to P4 (PW-8) to engage some labourers for cutting crop in his field and while returning from the house of P4, when the deceased came near the L1 of appellant No. 4, he shouted to the remaining accused who were there that the deceased was their enemy and he should not be allowed to go and kill him. The appellant No. 6 P5 assaulted the deceased with the lathi on his head. When he fell down on the ground, all the appellants assaulted him. In spite of P3 intervening, the appellants did not stop assaulting. When they found that the deceased had died, the appellants dragged his body from the spot to place near L2 of appellant No. 1 P6. Thereafter, they ran away. P7 (PW-10), brother of the deceased lodged first information report. The incident was witnessed by P4 (PW-8) and P8 (PW-13). The trial court did not believe the evidence of eye-witnesses P4 (PW-8) and P8 (PW-13); found certain discrepancies in the statements of witnesses; doubted the evidence of eye-witnesses to have identified the accused in darkness; consequently, the appellants were acquitted by the trial court giving benefit of doubt. On appeal by the State, the High Court upset the order of acquittal recorded by the trial court and held the appellants guilty of the offences and sentenced all the appellants to rigorous imprisonment for one year and for imprisonment of life. Both the sentences were to run concurrently. Hence, this appeal to this Court.
Žaliocima je suđeno za krivična djela na osnovu navoda da je oko četiri mjeseca prije događaja došlo do svađe između pokojnog P1 i žalioca br. 4 P2 u vezi sa podizanjem graničnog zida. Dana 12. aprila 1983. godine, oko 7.00 časova uveče, pokojni je u pratnji P3 otišao kod P4 (PW-8) da angažuje radnike za žetvu usjeva na svojoj njivi i, dok se vraćao iz kuće P4, kada je pokojni došao blizu L1 žalioca br. 4, ovaj je doviknuo ostalim optuženima koji su se tamo nalazili da je pokojni njihov neprijatelj i da mu ne smiju dozvoliti da ode, već da ga ubiju. Žalilac br. 6 P5 udario je pokojnog motkom (lathi) po glavi. Kada je pao na zemlju, svi žalioci su ga napali. Uprkos tome što je P3 intervenisao, žalioci nijesu prestali da ga napadaju. Kada su utvrdili da je pokojni preminuo, žalioci su odvukli njegovo tijelo sa lica mjesta do mjesta blizu L2 žalioca br. 1 P6. Nakon toga su pobjegli. P7 (PW-10), brat pokojnog, podnio je krivičnu prijavu. Događaju su kao očevici prisustvovali P4 (PW-8) i P8 (PW-13). Prvostepeni sud nije povjerovao iskazima svjedoka očevidaca P4 (PW-8) i P8 (PW-13); utvrdio je određene nedosljednosti u izjavama svjedoka; posumnjao je u iskaze svjedoka očevidaca da su prepoznali optužene u mraku; shodno tome, prvostepeni sud je oslobodio žalioce, dajući im korist sumnje. Po žalbi države, Visoki sud je preinačio oslobađajuću presudu koju je donio prvostepeni sud, oglasio žalioce krivim za krivična djela i osudio sve žalioce na kaznu strogog zatvora u trajanju od jedne godine i na kaznu doživotnog zatvora. Obje kazne izvršavaju se istovremeno. Stoga je izjavljena ova žalba ovom sudu.

In this appeal by special leave the sole appellant is P1 who alongwith six others was put up for trial before the Additional Sessions Judge in charged of the offence. It is not necessary to refer to the charges framed against the remaining accused since they are not appellants before us. The trial court by its judgment and order of April 24, 1995 found the appellant guilty of offence and sentenced him to undergo rigorous imprisonment for 10 years. The appellant preferred Criminal Appeal No.125 of 1995 before the High Court which was dismissed by the High Court by its impugned judgment and order of April 5, 2002. We may only observe that of the seven persons put up for trial before the learned Additional Sessions Judge one P2 was given the benefit of doubt and acquitted. One P3 was sentenced to life imprisonment and  P4 was sentenced to life imprisonment. The remaining accused were sentenced to 10 years rigorous imprisonment. The appeals preferred by the remaining accused have also been disposed of by the High Court by the impugned judgment. The case of the prosecution is that the informant Dr. D1 is a resident of L1. On January 13, 1989 at 2010 hours he lodged a first information report at police station in which he stated that on that date at about 7.30 p.m. while he was watching the television, other members of the family were in the house. His son P5, PW-1 had gone to the fields and had not returned. While he was watching the television he saw that three persons entered his house with concealed faces. Of them two were armed with pistols and they demanded the keys from him. He could identify accused P3 by his voice, stature and eyes. He then heard the cries of his daughter-in-law coming from another room and when he rushed to her room he found that two other persons were threatening her, of whom one was armed with country made pistol. His daughter-in-law handed over to them whatever ornaments she was wearing at that time. Two other persons then entered the room who picked up some articles. Those two persons had not concealed their faces. Some other dacoits also entered the room of his daughter-in-law and started making demands from her. A relative of his, namely P6, PW-4, who was residing with him, told his daughter-in-law to handover the keys to the dacoits. Thereafter the dacoits asked P6 to open the almirah but he was unable to do so. One of the dacoits threatened to kill him if he did not open the almirah. Seeing this, the informant rushed and caught that hand of the dacoit in which he was holding the pistol and pushed him towards the verandah. Thereafter the informant's daughter, P7 started raising alarm. Some of the dacoits were in the courtyard of the house and he recognized one of them as Rajesh Yadav who was armed with a pistol. Rajesh Yadav exhorted Parshuram to fire and thereafter Parshuram fired hitting his daughter P7. The dacoits also exploded bombs. The informant claimed to have recognized one of the miscreants as P2 who assaulted him on his back with the barrel of his pistol as a result of which his grip over one of the dacoits, whom he had caught, loosened and that dacoit slipped away. Thereafter the dacoits fled away. His daughter P7 succumbed to her injuries. By this time his son P5, PW-1 had also come. He mentioned in his report that P20, P6, PW-4 and P30, PW-2 were also injured. In the report he also gave descriptions of other dacoits whom he had not recognized.
U ovoj žalbi po posebnoj dozvoli jedini žalilac je P1, kojem je zajedno sa još šest lica suđeno pred dodatnim sudijom Sesijskog suda po optužbi za krivično djelo. Nije potrebno pozivati se na optužbe podignute protiv ostalih optuženih, budući da oni nijesu žalioci pred ovim sudom. Prvostepeni sud je presudom i rješenjem od 24. aprila 1995. godine oglasio žalioca krivim za krivično djelo i osudio ga na kaznu strogog zatvora u trajanju od 10 godina. Žalilac je izjavio krivičnu žalbu br. 125 iz 1995. godine Visokom sudu, koju je Visoki sud odbio pobijanom presudom i rješenjem od 5. aprila 2002. godine. Možemo samo primijetiti da je od sedam lica kojima je suđeno pred uvaženim dodatnim sudijom Sesijskog suda jednom licu, P2, data korist sumnje i da je ono oslobođeno. Jedan od njih, P3, osuđen je na kaznu doživotnog zatvora, a P4 je osuđen na kaznu doživotnog zatvora. Ostali optuženi osuđeni su na kaznu strogog zatvora u trajanju od 10 godina. Visoki sud je pobijanom presudom odlučio i o žalbama koje su izjavili ostali optuženi. Prema navodima optužbe, prijavilac dr D1 ima prebivalište u L1. Dana 13. januara 1989. godine u 2010 časova podnio je krivičnu prijavu u policijskoj stanici, u kojoj je naveo da su tog dana, oko 7.30 časova uveče, dok je on gledao televiziju, ostali članovi porodice bili u kući. Njegov sin P5, PW-1, otišao je u polje i nije se bio vratio. Dok je gledao televiziju, vidio je da su u njegovu kuću ušla tri maskirana lica. Dvojica od njih bili su naoružani pištoljima i od njega su zahtijevali ključeve. Optuženog P3 mogao je prepoznati po glasu, stasu i očima. Zatim je čuo krike svoje snahe iz druge sobe i, kada je pojurio u njenu sobu, zatekao je dva druga lica koja su joj prijetila, od kojih je jedno bilo naoružano pištoljem domaće izrade. Njegova snaha im je predala sav nakit koji je u tom trenutku nosila. Zatim su u sobu ušla još dva lica, koja su uzela neke predmete. Ta dva lica nijesu bila maskirana. U sobu njegove snahe ušli su i neki drugi razbojnici i počeli su da joj postavljaju zahtjeve. Njegov rođak, po imenu P6, PW-4, koji je živio sa njim, rekao je njegovoj snahi da preda ključeve razbojnicima. Nakon toga su razbojnici tražili od P6 da otvori ormar (almirah), ali on to nije mogao da učini. Jedan od razbojnika zaprijetio je da će ga ubiti ako ne otvori ormar. Vidjevši to, prijavilac je pojurio, uhvatio onu ruku razbojnika u kojoj je ovaj držao pištolj i gurnuo ga prema verandi. Nakon toga je kćerka prijavioca, P7, počela da zapomaže. Neki od razbojnika bili su u dvorištu kuće, a za jednog od njih, koji je bio naoružan pištoljem, prepoznao je da je to Rajesh Yadav. Rajesh Yadav je podsticao da Parshuram puca, nakon čega je Parshuram pucao i pogodio njegovu kćerku P7. Razbojnici su detonirali i bombe. Prijavilac je tvrdio da je jednog od napadača prepoznao kao P2, koji ga je udario po leđima cijevi svog pištolja, usljed čega je popustio njegov stisak nad jednim od razbojnika kojeg je bio uhvatio, pa se taj razbojnik izvukao. Nakon toga su razbojnici pobjegli. Njegova kćerka P7 podlegla je povredama. Do tada je došao i njegov sin P5, PW-1. U prijavi je naveo da su povrijeđeni i P20, P6, PW-4 i P30, PW-2. U prijavi je dao i opise ostalih razbojnika koje nije prepoznao.

Challenge in this appeal is to the judgment of the High Court upholding the conviction of the appellant for offences punishable and sentenced him to undergo imprisonment for life and also to pay a fine of Rs.1,000/- with default stipulations, as recorded by the Principal Sessions Judge.  The prosecution version, in a nutshell, is as follows. PW-1 is the wife of PW-4. The accused and PW-4 are the sons of P1 (hereinafter referred to as the 'deceased'). PW-2 is the son of PW-1. P1 had certain immovable properties which he partitioned 10 years before the occurrence, and he regained a piece of land namely 10 cents, which is a poramboke, for his livelihood. The accused was insisting him to give that land also. There arose a civil dispute between them. It also ended in favour of P1. On the day of occurrence i.e, 26.05.2003 at about 7.00 A.M., PW-1 was going to the garden to pluck vegetables. At that time, her father-in-law, the said P1, was cutting trees. He was having a spade and aruval in hand. At that time, the accused came there and questioned how he could cut the trees, and following the same, there was a wordy duel. Immediately, the accused snatched the aruval and cut him on the neck and shoulder indiscriminately. PW-1 on seeing this, raised alarm, and immediately, the accused fled away from the place of occurrence. The said P1 met his instantaneous death. PW-1 proceeded to the Police Station, where, the sub- Inspector of Police (PW-11), was present. PW-1 gave a report (Ex.P1), on the strength of which a case came to be registered. The first information report, Ex.P-12, along with Ex.P1 was despatched to the Magistrates' Court. The Inspector of Police (PW-12), on receipt of the copy of the FIR, took up investigation, proceeded to the spot, made an inspection in the presence of witnesses and prepared an observation mahazar, Ex.P-4, and a rough sketch, Ex.P-13. Then, he conducted inquest on the dead body of P1 in the presence of witnesses and panchayatdars and prepared an inquest report, Ex.P-14. The dead body was sent to the Government Hospital along with a requisition, Ex.P-2, for the purpose of autopsy. The Assistant Surgeon (PW-6), attached to the Government Hospital, on receipt of the said requisition, conducted autopsy on the dead body of P1 and found 7 cut injuries. The doctor gave a post- mortem certificate, Ex.P-3, with her opinion that the deceased would appear to have died of hemorrhage and shock due to injuries to major vessels. Pending the investigation, the Investigating Officer arrested the accused on 27.05.2003. He volunteered to give a confessional statement, which was recorded by the Investigator. The admissible part of the confession was marked as Ex. P-6, pursuant to which he produced M.O.-1, aruval and M.O.-4, Shirt, which have been recovered under a mahazar, Ex.P-7. The accused was sent for judicial remand. All the material objects recovered from the place of occurrence and from the dead body and M.Os. l and 4, recovered from the accused, were subjected to chemical analysis by the Forensic Sciences Department, which resulted in two reports namely Ex.P-10, the Chemical Analyst's report and Ex.P-11, the Serologist's report. On completion of investigation, the Investigator filed the final report. Charges were framed. The accused pleaded innocence. Twelve witnesses were examined to further the prosecution version. The accused, in his examination submitted that he has been falsely implicated and in any event, there was a wordy duel before the occurrence in which the appellant had purportedly snatched the weapon from the hands of the deceased and, therefore, a statute has no application. The Trial Court did not accept the plea and placing reliance on the evidence of the eye-witnesses, PWs-l and 2, recorded the conviction and sentence, as noted above. The plea taken before the Trial Court was reiterated by the accused persons before the High Court. By the impugned judgment, the High Court did not find any substance in the plea and dismissed the appeal.
Predmet pobijanja u ovoj žalbi je presuda Visokog suda kojom je potvrđena osuđujuća presuda glavnog sudije Sesijskog suda, kojom je žalilac oglašen krivim za kažnjiva krivična djela i osuđen na kaznu doživotnog zatvora, kao i na plaćanje novčane kazne od Rs.1,000/- uz odredbe za slučaj neplaćanja.  Verzija optužbe, ukratko, glasi kako slijedi. PW-1 je supruga PW-4. Optuženi i PW-4 su sinovi P1 (u daljem tekstu: pokojni). PW-2 je sin PW-1. P1 je imao određene nepokretnosti koje je podijelio 10 godina prije događaja, a za svoje izdržavanje povratio je komad zemljišta, i to 10 centi, koje je poramboke. Optuženi je insistirao da mu da i to zemljište. Između njih je nastao građanskopravni spor. I on je okončan u korist P1. Na dan događaja, tj. 26.05.2003. godine, oko 7.00 časova ujutru, PW-1 je išla u baštu da ubere povrće. U to vrijeme njen svekar, pomenuti P1, sjekao je drveće. U ruci je imao ašov i aruval. Tada je došao optuženi i upitao ga kako može da siječe drveće, nakon čega je došlo do verbalnog sukoba. Optuženi je odmah oteo aruval i nasumično ga sjekao po vratu i ramenu. Vidjevši to, PW-1 je počela da zapomaže, a optuženi je odmah pobjegao sa mjesta događaja. Pomenuti P1 je na licu mjesta preminuo. PW-1 je otišla u policijsku stanicu, gdje je bio prisutan podinspektor policije (PW-11). PW-1 je podnijela prijavu (Ex.P1), na osnovu koje je registrovan predmet. Krivična prijava, Ex.P-12, zajedno sa Ex.P1 dostavljena je sudu magistrata. Inspektor policije (PW-12), po prijemu kopije krivične prijave (FIR), preuzeo je istragu, otišao na lice mjesta, izvršio uviđaj u prisustvu svjedoka i sačinio zapisnik o uviđaju (mahazar), Ex.P-4, kao i skicu lica mjesta, Ex.P-13. Zatim je izvršio pregled leša P1 u prisustvu svjedoka i panchayatdars i sačinio izvještaj o pregledu leša, Ex.P-14. Leš je upućen u Državnu bolnicu, zajedno sa zahtjevom, Ex.P-2, radi obdukcije. Pomoćni hirurg (PW-6), zaposlena u Državnoj bolnici, po prijemu navedenog zahtjeva izvršila je obdukciju leša P1 i utvrdila 7 sječenih povreda. Ljekarka je izdala potvrdu o obdukciji, Ex.P-3, sa svojim mišljenjem da je pokojni, kako se čini, preminuo od krvarenja i šoka usljed povreda velikih krvnih sudova. Tokom istrage, službenik koji je vodio istragu uhapsio je optuženog 27.05.2003. godine. Optuženi je dobrovoljno dao priznanje, koje je zabilježio službenik koji vodi istragu. Dozvoljeni dio priznanja označen je kao Ex. P-6, na osnovu kojeg je optuženi predao M.O.-1, aruval, i M.O.-4, košulju, koji su oduzeti uz zapisnik (mahazar), Ex.P-7. Optuženi je upućen u sudski pritvor. Svi materijalni predmeti pronađeni na mjestu događaja i na lešu, kao i M.Os. l i 4 oduzeti od optuženog, podvrgnuti su hemijskoj analizi u Odjeljenju za forenzičke nauke, što je rezultiralo dvama izvještajima, i to Ex.P-10, izvještajem hemijskog analitičara, i Ex.P-11, izvještajem serologa. Po okončanju istrage, službenik koji vodi istragu podnio je završni izvještaj. Podignute su optužbe. Optuženi se izjasnio da nije kriv. Radi potkrepljenja verzije optužbe saslušano je dvanaest svjedoka. Optuženi je prilikom saslušanja naveo da je lažno okrivljen i da je, u svakom slučaju, prije događaja došlo do verbalnog sukoba u kojem je žalilac navodno oteo oružje iz ruku pokojnog, te da se stoga zakon ne primjenjuje. Prvostepeni sud nije prihvatio tu tvrdnju i, oslanjajući se na iskaze svjedoka očevidaca, PWs-l i 2, izrekao je osuđujuću presudu i kaznu, kako je prethodno navedeno. Tvrdnju istaknutu pred prvostepenim sudom optuženi su ponovili pred Visokim sudom. Pobijanom presudom Visoki sud nije našao da je ta tvrdnja osnovana i odbio je žalbu.
queries · 00047
Whether sanction is required to initiate criminal proceedings in respect of offences is the question arising for consideration in these cases. The District Registrar lodged a complaint with the Inspector of Police, CBCID on 07.07.1999. The main allegation against the respondents was that while they were working as Sub-Registrars in various offices in the State, they conspired with stamp vendors and document writers and other staff to gain monetary benefit and resorted to manipulation of registers and got the registration of the documents with old value of the properties, resulting in wrongful gain to themselves and loss to the Government, and thereby cheated the public and the Government. On the basis of the complaint, F.I.R. No. 35/1999 was registered by the appellant, and after investigation, report against 41 persons including the respondents herein, was submitted before the III Additional Chief Metropolitan Magistrate. The respondents raised the objection that there was no sanction and hence the proceedings could not be initiated. Learned Magistrate on 03.07.2007 passed an order holding that: "Whether the sanction is required or not to be considered during the trial and it is the burden on the complainant to prove that the accused acted beyond in discharge of their official duties and there is no nexus between the acts committed and their official duties and at this stage the question that the accused acted within their duties cannot be decided." Aggrieved, respondents moved the High Court leading to the impugned order whereby the criminal proceedings were quashed on the sole ground that there was no sanction, and hence the appeals.
Pitanje koje se razmatra u ovim predmetima jeste da li je potrebno odobrenje za pokretanje krivičnog postupka u pogledu krivičnih djela. Okružni registrator podnio je 07.07.1999. godine prijavu inspektoru policije, CBCID. Glavni navod protiv protivnih strana bio je da su, dok su radili kao pomoćnici registratora u raznim kancelarijama u državi, sklopili dogovor sa prodavcima taksenih marki, pisarima isprava i drugim osobljem radi sticanja novčane koristi, pribjegli manipulaciji registrima i obezbijedili registraciju isprava sa starom vrijednošću imovine, što je dovelo do protivpravne imovinske koristi za njih same i protivpravne štete za Vladu, i time prevarili javnost i Vladu. Na osnovu prijave, žalilac je registrovao krivičnu prijavu (F.I.R.) br. 35/1999 i, nakon istrage, izvještaj protiv 41 lica, uključujući ovdje navedene protivne strane, podnesen je III dodatnom glavnom metropolitanskom magistratu. Protivne strane su istakle prigovor da nije bilo odobrenja i da se stoga postupak nije mogao pokrenuti. Uvaženi magistrat je 03.07.2007. godine donio rješenje u kojem je zauzeo stav: „Da li je odobrenje potrebno ili ne treba razmotriti tokom suđenja i teret je na podnosiocu prijave da dokaže da su okrivljeni postupali izvan vršenja svojih službenih dužnosti i da ne postoji veza između izvršenih radnji i njihovih službenih dužnosti, a u ovoj fazi ne može se odlučiti o pitanju da li su okrivljeni postupali u okviru svojih dužnosti.“ Nezadovoljne time, protivne strane su se obratile Visokom sudu, što je dovelo do pobijanog rješenja kojim je krivični postupak ukinut isključivo po osnovu toga što nije bilo odobrenja, pa su stoga izjavljene ove žalbe.

Appellant was a Patwari working at village V1 in the year 1976. On an allegation that he had sought illegal gratification, on or about 13 July 1976, a complaint was lodged in the office of Deputy Superintendent of Police, Anti-Corruption that the appellant had asked for illegal gratification. A raiding party laid a trap on the said date and he was found to have accepted illegal gratification. Pursuant thereto he was prosecuted for alleged commission of an offence. He was placed under suspension. He was convicted by reason of a judgment dated 25 February 1985 passed by the Special Judge (A.C.D.). He was dismissed from service in terms of the said judgment of conviction by an order dated 3 October 1987 2. The appellant preferred an appeal against the said judgment of conviction and sentence and by reason of a judgment and order dated 16 January 2001, the said appeal was allowed. The appellant, thus, stood acquitted. In the meanwhile, i.e., in the year 1998, the appellant reached his age of superannuation. Having been acquitted in the criminal proceeding, he filed a writ petition before the High Court which. By an order dated 19 February 2003, a learned Single Judge of the High Court directed that in the event the appellant files a representation before the competent officer with regard to pension, the same may be considered within a period of three months therefrom. An appeal preferred there against was dismissed by reason of the impugned order passed by the Division Bench.
Žalilac je 1976. godine radio kao Patwari u selu V1. Po navodu da je tražio nedozvoljenu korist, 13. jula 1976. godine ili približno tog datuma podnesena je prijava kancelariji zamjenika načelnika policije, Odjeljenje za borbu protiv korupcije, da je žalilac tražio nedozvoljenu korist. Ekipa za hvatanje na djelu (raiding party) je navedenog dana postavila zamku i utvrđeno je da je on primio nedozvoljenu korist. Shodno tome, protiv njega je pokrenut krivični postupak zbog navodnog izvršenja krivičnog djela. Suspendovan je. Oglašen je krivim presudom od 25. februara 1985. godine koju je donio specijalni sudija (A.C.D.). Otpušten je iz službe u skladu sa navedenom osuđujućom presudom rješenjem od 3. oktobra 1987. godine 2. Žalilac je izjavio žalbu protiv navedene osuđujuće presude i kazne i presudom i rješenjem od 16. januara 2001. godine navedena žalba je usvojena. Žalilac je, dakle, oslobođen. U međuvremenu, tj. 1998. godine, žalilac je navršio starosnu granicu za penzionisanje. Pošto je oslobođen u krivičnom postupku, podnio je ustavnu tužbu (writ petition) Visokom sudu koji. Rješenjem od 19. februara 2003. godine uvaženi sudija pojedinac Visokog suda naložio je da se, u slučaju da žalilac podnese predstavku nadležnom službeniku u vezi sa penzijom, ona razmotri u roku od tri mjeseca od tada. Žalba izjavljena protiv toga odbijena je pobijanim rješenjem koje je donijelo vijeće (Division Bench).
queries · 00049
A peculiar feature of this appeal by special leave is that it is not an appeal against conviction or against acquittal but one preferred by a prosecution witness for expunction of several highly derogatory remarks made against him by a learned Judge of the High Court while allowing a Criminal Appeal at the High Court. P1, the appellant before us was examined as P.W. 8 in the trial of a case on the file of the Special Judge (Vigilance) against the first respondent. The trial ended in conviction against the first respondent and when the appeal filed by him came to be heard by the High Court the appellant had become a Cabinet Minister in the State. On account of the disparaging remarks made by the Appellate Judge the appellant tendered his resignation and demitted office for maintaining democratic traditions. It is in that backgroud this appeal has come to be preferred. Pursuant to a trap laid by the Vigilance Police on the complaint of the appellant's Manager, P2 (P.W.2) the first respondent was arrested on 26.4.79 for having accepted a bribe of Rs. 2,000 from P2. The marked currency notes M.Os. V to XXVI were recovered from the brief case M.O. II of the first respondent prior to the arrest. The prosecution case was that the first respondent had been extracting illegal gratification at the rate of Rs. 1,000 er month during the months of January, February and March, 1979 from P2 but all of a sudden he raised the demand to Rs. 2,000 per month in April 1979 and this led to P2 laying information (Exhibit I) before the Superintendent of Police (Vigilance). Acting on the report, a trap was laid on 26.4.79 and after P2 had handed over the marked currency notes the Vigilance party entered the office and recovered the currency notes from the brief case and arrested the first respondent. The first respondent denied having received any illegal gratification but offered no explanation for the presence of the currency notes in his brief case. Eleven witnesses including the appellant who figured as P.W.8 were examined by the prosecution and the first respondent examined three witnesses D.Ws. 1 to 3 to substantiate the defence set up by him, viz., that the sum of Rs. 2,000 had been paid by way of donation for conducting a drama and publishing a souvenir by the Mining Officers' Club and also towards donation for Children's Welfare Fund. The Special Judge accepted the prosecution case and held the first respondent guilty. The Special Judge awarded a sentence of rigorous imprisonment for one year for the conviction under the first charge but did not award any separate sentence for the second  conviction. Against the conviction and sentence the first respondent preferred Criminal Appeal No. 31 of 1982 to the High Court. A learned Judge of the High Court has allowed the appeal holding that the prosecution has not proved its case by acceptable evidence and besides, the first respondent's explanation for the possession of the currency notes appeared probable. While acquitting the first respondent the learned Judge has, however, made several adverse remarks about the conduct of the appellant and about the credibility of his testimony and it is with that part of the judgment we are now concerned with in this appeal.
Osobenost ove žalbe izjavljene na osnovu posebne dozvole jeste u tome što ona nije izjavljena protiv osuđujuće niti protiv oslobađajuće presude, već ju je izjavio svjedok optužbe radi brisanja više krajnje pogrdnih primjedbi koje je o njemu iznio uvaženi sudija Visokog suda prilikom usvajanja krivične žalbe pred Visokim sudom. P1, žalilac pred ovim sudom, saslušan je kao P.W. 8 na suđenju u predmetu koji se vodio pred specijalnim sudijom (Vigilance) protiv prve protivne strane. Suđenje je okončano osuđujućom presudom protiv prve protivne strane, a kada je Visoki sud razmatrao žalbu koju je ona izjavila, žalilac je u međuvremenu postao ministar u Vladi države. Zbog omalovažavajućih primjedbi koje je iznio sudija u žalbenom postupku, žalilac je podnio ostavku i napustio funkciju radi očuvanja demokratskih tradicija. Upravo je u tim okolnostima izjavljena ova žalba. Na osnovu zamke koju je postavila Vigilance policija po prijavi žaliočevog upravnika, P2 (P.W.2), prva protivna strana je uhapšena 26. aprila 1979. godine zbog toga što je od P2 primila mito u iznosu od Rs. 2,000. Obilježene novčanice M.Os. V do XXVI pronađene su i oduzete iz aktovke M.O. II prve protivne strane prije hapšenja. Prema navodima optužbe, prva protivna strana je tokom januara, februara i marta 1979. godine od P2 iznuđivala nedozvoljenu korist u iznosu od Rs. 1,000 mjesečno, ali je u aprilu 1979. godine iznenada povećala zahtjev na Rs. 2,000 mjesečno, što je navelo P2 da podnese prijavu (dokazni predmet I) nadzorniku policije (Vigilance). Postupajući po prijavi, 26. aprila 1979. godine postavljena je zamka i, nakon što je P2 predao obilježene novčanice, ekipa Vigilance policije ušla je u kancelariju, pronašla novčanice u aktovci i uhapsila prvu protivnu stranu. Prva protivna strana je poricala da je primila bilo kakvu nedozvoljenu korist, ali nije dala nikakvo objašnjenje za prisustvo novčanica u svojoj aktovci. Optužba je saslušala jedanaest svjedoka, uključujući žalioca, koji se pojavio kao P.W.8, a prva protivna strana je saslušala tri svjedoka, D.Ws. 1 do 3, kako bi potkrijepila odbranu koju je istakla, odnosno da je iznos od Rs. 2,000 plaćen kao donacija za izvođenje drame i objavljivanje prigodne publikacije Kluba službenika rudarstva, kao i kao donacija Fondu za dobrobit djece. Specijalni sudija je prihvatio navode optužbe i oglasio prvu protivnu stranu krivom. Specijalni sudija je izrekao kaznu strogog zatvora u trajanju od jedne godine za osuđujuću presudu po prvoj tački optužbe, ali nije izrekao posebnu kaznu za drugu osuđujuću presudu. Protiv osuđujuće presude i kazne prva protivna strana je izjavila Visokom sudu krivičnu žalbu br. 31 iz 1982. godine. Uvaženi sudija Visokog suda usvojio je žalbu, zaključivši da optužba nije dokazala svoje navode prihvatljivim dokazima i da je, osim toga, objašnjenje prve protivne strane u pogledu posjedovanja novčanica djelovalo vjerovatno. Međutim, oslobađajući prvu protivnu stranu, uvaženi sudija je iznio više nepovoljnih primjedbi o ponašanju žalioca i o vjerodostojnosti njegovog iskaza, i upravo se tim dijelom presude bavimo u ovoj žalbi.
